# 第6級第四批｜6B-13《從今天起，太陽需要每月訂閱》

**Sunlight Is Now a Subscription**

**類型：** 企業反烏托邦／能源政治  
**級距：** Level 6（5000–6000 字彙）  
**版本：** 拾頁 StorySteps｜第四批｜25 章完整教學初稿  
**正式配詞：** L6_B本_25章配詞.md，501 個原始 ID

## 故事簡介

軌道反射鏡使陽光可以控制後，企業開始向每戶家庭按分鐘收費。市政技師奈拉看著兒童醫院上空的太陽被關掉，卻發現陽光從不曾真正短缺。

**English premise:** After orbital mirrors make sunlight controllable, a corporation begins charging every family by the minute. Municipal technician Nara watches the sun go out over a children’s hospital—and finds that the sunlight was never scarce.

## 世界規則

軌道反射鏡只能改變陽光方向，不能創造陽光。熱浪時移除所有遮光可能和冬天斷光一樣危險，所以安全方案必須是透明控制，而非魯莽停機。

**English:** Orbital mirrors could redirect sunlight, never create it. Removing all shade in a heat wave could be as dangerous as withholding light in winter, so a safe solution required transparent control rather than a reckless shutdown.

## 閱讀方法與編輯說明

每一個 EN 英文段落後緊接繁中對照段落；詞彙證據句須出現在同章英文正文中。部分高階配詞取自本專案既有教學語料，依新題材整理，**尚未完成逐句文學編輯及語意終校**。本書為完整章節的教學初稿，並非出版定稿。第10章設有核心反轉，第25章主線收束。中文詞義依情境，不代表配詞原檔官方翻譯。

---

## CHAPTER 01｜The Morning the Sun Asked for Money｜太陽索取金錢的早晨

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：620**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The sun rose over Nara’s apartment for precisely eight seconds. Then her window turned black and displayed a payment screen. PLEASE RENEW YOUR DAYLIGHT SUBSCRIPTION. Across the street, a mother holding a fox cub toy stood beside the pediatric clinic, begging for a little light. The nursery’s plants were already bending toward an emergency lamp. Nara worked for the municipal light department; she knew that the orbital mirrors were still full of sunshine. The clasp on her service meter was warm from the current. What had been removed was not the sun but permission to receive it.

**繁中** 陽光在奈拉公寓上方升起，精確地照了八秒，接著窗戶轉黑，跳出付款畫面：「請續訂日照服務。」街對面，一位拿著小狐狸幼獸玩偶的母親站在兒科診所旁，乞求一點光。育嬰室的植物早已彎向緊急照明燈。奈拉在市政日照部門工作，知道軌道反射鏡依然照滿陽光。她的公務計量器扣環因電流而發熱。被移除的不是太陽，而是接收陽光的許可。

**EN** At nine, the company announced a new directive: every dwelling would receive seven free minutes of daylight and could purchase the rest. A commentator called the old mayor a dictator for having required a universal right to the sky. Director Solven prepared to convene a ceremony celebrating the market reform. Nara read the official motto, “A Fair Share of Every Dawn,” and wondered whether the phrase fair share had become a synonym for what people could afford. A janitor cleaning the transit roof told her that he used to watch sunlight gallop along the metal rails before the new shade panels came down.

**繁中** 九點，公司公布新指令：每處住所每日可得七分鐘免費日照，其餘必須購買。評論員稱前市長是獨裁者，因為他曾要求人人擁有接觸天空的權利。索爾文總監正準備召開市場改革慶典。奈拉讀著官方座右銘「公平分享每一道黎明」，心想「公平分享」是否已成了「有錢買得到」的同義詞。清潔員在交通站屋頂告訴她，以前他會看陽光沿金屬軌道奔馳，直到新遮光板降下。

**EN** Nara carried an old optical meter to the clinic’s dental wing, where children were scheduled for treatment that required a stable light cycle. The meter remained stationary on its stand, but the readings above the shadow layer were normal. A hospital trustee offered her unconditional access to the building if she would promise to keep the patients’ identities private. She agreed. There was no legal precedent, she realized, for letting a private firm decide which medical procedure deserved morning light. She photographed the readings instead of making a speech. An accusation without proof would only heighten the parents’ anxiety.

**繁中** 奈拉把舊光度計帶到診所牙科區，孩子們正在接受需要穩定光週期的治療。計量器穩穩放在支架上，遮光層上方的讀數卻完全正常。醫院受託人表示，只要她承諾保護病人身分，就無條件開放建物供查驗。她答應了。她忽然明白，從沒有法律先例允許私人公司決定哪一種醫療程序配得到晨光。她先拍下讀數，沒有發表演說。沒有證據的指控只會加劇家長的焦慮。

**EN** Former mirror pilot Eli met her beside a community garden that had begun to die. A gardener was trying to transplant seedlings into movable containers so they could chase stray beams of light. “They tell us we will reap more efficiency,” Eli said, “but the system throws away most of what the sky provides.” He had flown the mirrors when the array was built. The design could redirect sunlight but never create it. If the corporation had truly run out of available light, its reserve meter would have fallen. Instead it was rising. Eli handed Nara a copy of the orbital allocation log and asked her to check the figures independently.

**繁中** 前鏡陣駕駛伊萊在逐漸枯萎的社區花園旁與她碰面。園丁正試圖把幼苗移植進可移動容器，好追逐零星光束。「他們說我們會收穫更高效率，」伊萊說，「系統卻丟棄天空供給的大部分資源。」鏡陣建造時，他曾負責駕駛。他知道設計只能轉向陽光，不能創造陽光。若公司真的沒有可用光量，備用計量應下降，然而數值卻在升高。伊萊把軌道分配日誌交給奈拉，請她獨立核對。

**EN** By afternoon, a traveler described herself as a pilgrim of the first dawn. She had walked for days from an unregistered district, carrying a picture of a sun that children there had never seen. Nara listened while Solven’s publicity speakers promised that subscriptions would make the entire city flourish. The traveler did not ask for a free luxury; she asked why an ordinary sunrise had been defined as a luxury at all. Nara sent a copy of the meter reading to the public health board. Her report did not demand that every mirror stop turning. It demanded that the company identify who had ordered the shadows and on what authority.

**繁中** 下午，一名旅人自稱「第一道黎明的朝聖者」。她從未登記的街區徒步數日，帶來一張當地孩子從沒看過的太陽照片。奈拉聆聽她說話，索爾文的宣傳喇叭卻仍保證訂閱會使全城繁榮。旅人不是索取免費奢侈品，而是問平凡日出為何被定義成奢侈。奈拉把讀數送交公共衛生委員會。她的報告並未要求所有反射鏡停轉，而是要求公司說明誰有權下令製造陰影，以及依據何在。

**EN** At dusk, a service drone arrived with a notice informing Nara that her technician badge would be revoked unless she uttered a formal apology. She read the notice twice. Beneath the threat was a line of financial data that did not belong on an employee warning: PREPAID MILITARY LIGHT RESERVE—FULL. Eli looked at the number and went pale. Above them, the last narrow stripe of daylight crossed the abandoned school roof. “We are not running out of sunlight,” Nara said. “Somebody is keeping it where the invoices cannot reach.” The drone requested her signature. She left the box blank.

**繁中** 黃昏時，勤務無人機送來通知：奈拉若不正式說出道歉詞，技師證就會遭撤銷。她讀了兩遍。威脅下方竟有一行不該出現在員工警告上的財務資料：「預付軍用日照儲備——滿額。」伊萊看了數字便臉色發白。最後一道狹長陽光越過廢棄學校的屋頂。「我們沒有用完陽光，」奈拉說，「有人把它藏在帳單碰不到的地方。」無人機要求她簽名。她讓欄位保持空白。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. At sunrise, every window in Nara's district displays a payment demand, and a hospital nursery is left in shadow.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 日出時，奈拉所在街區每扇窗戶都跳出付款要求，醫院育嬰室陷入陰影。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The orbiting mirrors are still collecting sunlight; only the neighborhood’s distribution permission has been suspended.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 軌道反射鏡仍在收集陽光，停止的只是街區的日照配送許可。


## CHAPTER 02｜Seven Free Minutes｜七分鐘免費額度

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：898**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Director Solven offers each household seven free minutes of sun per day, calling it a generous safety measure. Nara asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。索爾文總監宣布每戶每日免費七分鐘日照，稱之為慷慨的安全措施。奈拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** Orbital mirrors could redirect sunlight, never create it. Removing all shade in a heat wave could be as dangerous as withholding light in winter, so a safe solution required transparent control rather than a reckless shutdown.

**繁中** 軌道反射鏡只能改變陽光方向，不能創造陽光。熱浪時移除所有遮光可能和冬天斷光一樣危險，所以安全方案必須是透明控制，而非魯莽停機。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Nara asked Eli not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。奈拉請伊萊不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Nara promised to abide by the clinic's consent rules even while helping Nara preserve the invoice. The director would not affirm the official opening date without seeing the original ledger. An alligator mascot on the old lesson chart reminded children to keep their distance. An old recording played a low bass note that made Jun remember the arrival of a train. The children's colloquial word for the forbidden street survived in a playground counting rhyme. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 奈拉承諾遵守診所的同意規則，同時協助奈拉保存發票。 主任沒看過原始帳冊，不願確認官方啟用日期。 舊課程圖表上的鱷魚吉祥物提醒孩子保持距離。 舊錄音響起低沉的低音，讓小鈞想起列車進站。 孩子口語中稱呼禁忌街道的詞，仍留在遊戲數數歌裡。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Nara measures a school garden that needs longer light cycles to keep its food plants alive. Nara watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Eli recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。奈拉測出學校菜園需要更長光週期才能讓作物活下去。奈拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。伊萊公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Treating forgotten dreams as commonplace noise was part of the clinic's training manual. The hospital's exemption would expire at midnight, giving them only hours to examine the records. Ivo discovered that an old fuse had protected the basement archive from the memory relay network. Nara offered quiet hospitality to worried parents, never asking for their children's forbidden dream details. The authorities called the visions infectious, although the clinic could provide no evidence of transmission. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 把被遺忘的夢當成尋常雜訊，是診所訓練手冊的一部分。 醫院的特別許可將在午夜到期，留給他們查閱紀錄的時間只剩數小時。 伊沃發現一枚舊保險絲，讓地下檔案室沒有接上記憶中繼網路。 奈拉溫和接待焦慮的家長，從不逼問孩子的禁忌夢境內容。 當局稱那些幻象有傳染性，但診所拿不出任何傳播證據。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Nara insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Eli drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。奈拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。伊萊畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A liter of cleaning fluid had spilled over the paper archive, obscuring several names but not the stamps. A novice technician had left the storage sensor disconnected, accidentally preserving the original poster. Nara considered it rash to accuse the clinic staff before she understood who controlled their systems. The old invoice and the official date could not reconcile without an independent explanation. The shabby ledger cabinet looked worthless, which was probably why nobody had removed it. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一公升清潔液灑在紙本檔案上，模糊了幾個姓名，印章卻還清楚。 一名新手技師曾讓儲藏室感測器保持斷線，意外保存了原始海報。 奈拉認為尚未查清誰控制系統，就指責診所員工太過魯莽。 沒有獨立說明，舊發票與官方日期便無法相互調和。 破舊的帳冊櫃看起來毫無價值，可能因此沒人搬走。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A borrowed spotlight made the presentation easier to see but obscured an exit sign. A volunteer brought ginger syrup for workers who had spent the afternoon in the cold. The handrail began to tilt when the tester leaned against its outer edge. The artificial twilight outside the clinic concealed the fact that no real sun had reached the street. An old label marked whiskey lay in the storeroom beside the railway invoices, dated before the city's founding. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 借來的聚光燈讓簡報更清楚，卻遮住了逃生標誌。 志工帶來薑糖漿，慰勞在寒風中工作一下午的人。 測試者靠在外側時，扶手開始傾斜。 診所外的人造暮光掩蓋了真實太陽根本照不到街道的事實。 一張印著威士忌的舊標籤躺在儲藏室鐵路發票旁，日期早於官方建城日。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Nara passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Eli paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，奈拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。伊萊停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 本章新目標詞

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Director Solven offers each household seven free minutes of sun per day, calling it a generous safety measure.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 索爾文總監宣布每戶每日免費七分鐘日照，稱之為慷慨的安全措施。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. Nara measures a school garden that needs longer light cycles to keep its food plants alive.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 奈拉測出學校菜園需要更長光週期才能讓作物活下去。


## CHAPTER 03｜The Woman Who Owns a Shadow｜擁有影子的女人

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：880**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. A landlord advertises apartments with guaranteed light while evicting families whose subscription has expired. Nara checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。房東宣傳保證日照的公寓，卻驅逐訂閱到期的家庭。奈拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Orbital mirrors could redirect sunlight, never create it. Removing all shade in a heat wave could be as dangerous as withholding light in winter, so a safe solution required transparent control rather than a reckless shutdown.

**繁中** 軌道反射鏡只能改變陽光方向，不能創造陽光。熱浪時移除所有遮光可能和冬天斷光一樣危險，所以安全方案必須是透明控制，而非魯莽停機。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Nara listened longer than the schedule allowed. Eli reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。奈拉聽得比議程允許的更久。伊萊提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The cashier's handwritten signature on the toy receipt survived two rounds of official records cleaning. A routine checkup form showed that the children were healthy before the forced relocation. Police claimed the midnight searches would curb public panic, but the raids only frightened residents. The railway alarm could deafen passengers standing too close to the old tunnel speakers. The Ministry labeled the market vendor a disgrace for keeping receipts beyond the retention period. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 玩具收據上收銀員的親筆簽名，熬過了兩輪官方檔案清理。 例行健康檢查表顯示，孩子們在強制搬遷前身體健康。 警方宣稱午夜搜查能遏止恐慌，突襲卻只讓居民更加害怕。 鐵路警報足以震聾站在舊隧道喇叭旁的乘客。 部會把超過保存期限還留著收據的攤販視為恥辱。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. One tenant produces a century-old deed promising access to the open sky. Nara read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Eli did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。一名房客拿出百年前的地契，載明住戶有權接觸開放天空。奈拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。伊萊沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The crew documented the safe disposal of contaminated fuel instead of pouring it into the tidewater behind the station. Nara recorded the vendor's dissent when officials tried to seize her original account book. The public register claimed the families had chosen to emigrate, yet none had signed a departure form. The false relocation policy did nothing to enrich the children whose school materials were confiscated. A lengthy transport invoice listed train journeys that were absent from the official timetable. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 船員記錄受污染燃料的安全處置，沒有把它倒進站後海水裡。 官員試圖扣押原始帳本時，奈拉記下了攤販的異議。 公共戶籍聲稱這些家庭自願移居他處，卻沒有一戶簽過離境表。 錯誤的搬遷政策沒有改善孩子生活，反而沒收了他們的學習用品。 一張冗長運輸發票列出官方時刻表上不存在的列車旅程。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Solven demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Nara replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當索爾文總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。奈拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: For a lifelong resident, the changed light was more than a technical issue; it altered familiar night routes. The clerk addressed the elderly witness as madam and waited for her complete answer. A melancholy railway tune returned to the vendor whenever rain struck the market roof. The city's rules made it permissible to erase receipts if they contradicted an approved history. Eli had no wish to preach about courage to people whose jobs were at risk. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 對一名終生住在島上的居民而言，變動的燈光不只是技術問題，也改變熟悉的夜間路線。 書記尊稱那位年長證人為女士，並等她說完。 每當雨打在市場屋頂，攤販便想起一段憂傷的鐵路旋律。 城市規定允許銷毀與核准歷史矛盾的收據。 言秋不想向生計受威脅的人說教勇氣。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The smell of wet stone was reminiscent of a street Nara had been ordered to forget. There was no rivalry between Nara and Ivo; they needed different skills to verify the same documents. The suspicious transport account deserved scrutiny from an independent auditor, not an instant accusation. The waiting room was full of suspense before the safety officer spoke. The old railway trademark on a receipt matched the symbol painted beneath the clinic's animals. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 濕石頭的氣味讓奈拉想起一條被命令忘記的街道。 奈拉與伊沃之間沒有競爭，他們需要不同專長來核實同一批文件。 可疑的運輸帳戶需要獨立稽核員仔細審查，而不是立刻定罪。 安全官發言前，等候室充滿懸念。 收據上的舊鐵路商標，與診所動物壁畫底下的符號吻合。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Nara resisted the urge to call it proof. Eli opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。奈拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。伊萊翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. A landlord advertises apartments with guaranteed light while evicting families whose subscription has expired.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 房東宣傳保證日照的公寓，卻驅逐訂閱到期的家庭。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. One tenant produces a century-old deed promising access to the open sky.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 一名房客拿出百年前的地契，載明住戶有權接觸開放天空。


## CHAPTER 04｜The Technician's Forbidden Tool｜技師的禁用工具

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：866**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Nara repairs an old light meter that measures actual photons instead of company billing codes. Nara knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。奈拉修好一只老式光度計，測量真正的光子而不是企業帳單代碼。奈拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** Eli arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Nara could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 伊萊帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。奈拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The librarian posted a book about digital addiction beside a guide to balanced screen time. The clean aesthetic of the new galleries concealed the violence of their missing captions. The planner could give only an approximate count of attendees. The curator called his unauthorized work urban archaeology because ordinary history departments refused the materials. Eli wore a temporary keeper's badge, taking care not to mistake the emblem for unlimited authority. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 館員把數位成癮的書和健康使用螢幕指南並排展示。 新展廳整潔的美學風格，掩蓋了遭刪除說明所代表的暴力。 規劃者只能提供來賓人數的大約估計。 館長稱自己的未授權工作是都市考古學，因為正規歷史部門拒收這些資料。 言秋佩戴臨時守護員徽章，謹慎提醒自己這個標誌不等於無限權力。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. The meter reveals that dark districts are receiving enough light above the company’s artificial shade layer. For a long moment no one spoke. Nara understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Eli broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。儀表顯示，陰暗街區在企業遮光層上方其實有足夠陽光。一時之間無人開口。奈拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。伊萊打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The official account mentioned a great blaze, although the station's stone walls showed no fire damage. One young disciple of the state historian copied his claims without checking a single photograph. Nara hoped the exhibition might enlighten visitors, but she refused to replace facts with another comforting myth. The guard made a public fuss about camera rules to distract an officer from Eli's interview. The new museum label carried an implicit threat: remembering the wrong street made someone suspicious. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 官方記載提到一場大火，車站石牆卻沒有任何火損。 一名國家史官的年輕門生照抄他的說法，連一張照片都沒核對。 奈拉希望展覽能啟發參觀者，卻拒絕以另一套安慰人心的神話取代事實。 警衛故意大聲為攝影規定爭論，好分散警員對莎拉訪談的注意。 新館藏標籤暗含威脅：記得錯誤街道的人會遭到懷疑。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Nara had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Eli proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 奈拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。伊萊提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A government historian would preside over the reopening ceremony, though he had never inspected the archive. The museum's prestige depended on public trust, not simply on royal-looking seals and elegant lighting. The council planned to privatize the old station land before anyone could establish its rightful owners. Cold white lights appeared to radiate purity, but their brightness made the erased faces harder to see. The lifeguard rotation had to cover every permitted training hour. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一名從未檢查檔案的政府史官，卻將主持重新開幕典禮。 博物館的聲望來自公眾信任，不是華麗印章與燈光。 議會計畫在確定土地合法所有人之前，把舊車站用地私有化。 冷白燈光似乎散發純淨感，亮度卻讓被擦掉的面孔更難辨認。 救生員輪班必須涵蓋每個核准訓練時段。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: An old electric shaver displayed beside the photographs carried a manufacturer's address from Orchard East. A stray note beneath the glass included the route number of the missing passenger train. Official captions placed a veil of respectable language over the fact that whole families had vanished. A photograph of a vineyard outside the wall contradicted the government's claim that no crops survived there. The contractor's warranty still covered the sealed valve, proving the tunnel had been serviced recently. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 照片旁展示的老式電鬍刀，標著東果園的製造商地址。 玻璃展示櫃下夾著一張零散紙條，上面寫著失蹤客車的路線編號。 官方說明以體面措辭掩蓋了整個家庭失蹤的事實。 城牆外葡萄園的照片，反駁了政府宣稱外面沒有作物存活的說法。 承包商的保固仍涵蓋封閉閥門，證明隧道不久前有人維修。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Nara returned to an unpaid sunlight meter. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Eli remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the ring-shadow city waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，奈拉再次看向一只欠費的陽光計量器。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。伊萊守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的環影城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. Nara repairs an old light meter that measures actual photons instead of company billing codes.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 奈拉修好一只老式光度計，測量真正的光子而不是企業帳單代碼。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The meter reveals that dark districts are receiving enough light above the company’s artificial shade layer.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 儀表顯示，陰暗街區在企業遮光層上方其實有足夠陽光。


## CHAPTER 05｜The Warmth Auction｜溫暖拍賣會

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：849**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. The corporation auctions an extra hour of winter daylight to the highest-bidding neighborhoods. Nara had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the ring-shadow city, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。公司把冬季多一小時日照拍賣給出價最高的街區。奈拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在環影城，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Nara asked Eli to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。奈拉請伊萊說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The consultant treated the broken sensor as an accessory, though Nara knew it was vital evidence. The archived report described a breakdown in the relay safeguards during the first citywide memory trial. Ivo could bypass the shutter motor, but he refused to endanger the receptionist without asking. A photograph showed officials raising champagne glasses beside the first experimental sleep relay. The paper archive smelled faintly of chestnut polish, a scent Eli associated with her mother's workbench. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 顧問把損壞感測器當成小配件，奈拉卻知道它是重要證據。 封存報告描述第一次全城記憶測試時，中繼器的安全機制曾故障。 伊沃能繞過百葉窗馬達，卻拒絕未經詢問就使接待員陷入危險。 一張照片裡，官員在第一座實驗性睡眠中繼器旁舉起香檳杯。 紙本檔案帶著淡淡栗木蠟氣味，讓言秋想起母親的工作桌。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Eli, a former mirror pilot, refuses to steer the array toward luxury towers while emergency shelters freeze. Nara felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。前反射鏡駕駛伊萊拒絕讓鏡陣朝向豪華大樓，任由避難所凍結。奈拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Vale called the safety engineers cowardly because they had refused to approve a dangerous expansion. A blue thread tied to the warning sheet could evoke Nara's childhood memory of her mother's hands. The contractor's abandoned cafeteria still displayed a menu for roast fowl from the founding banquet. The receptionist's voice grew hoarse as she explained how many records were scheduled for destruction. Nara recognized the citrus lotion her mother had used from a stain inside the old engineer's glove. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 維爾說安全工程師膽小，只因他們拒絕批准危險擴建。 警告紙上綁著的藍線，能喚起奈拉對母親雙手的童年記憶。 承包商廢棄的餐廳仍展示建城宴席的烤禽類菜單。 接待員解釋即將銷毀多少紀錄時，聲音變得沙啞。 奈拉從舊工程手套內側的污漬，認出母親常用的柑橘乳液。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Solven and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Nara rejected both. The issue was whether a private company may sell access to the natural sky, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住索爾文總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。奈拉兩者都拒絕。核心是私人企業是否有權出售人們接觸自然天空的機會，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The records exposed a dangerous mentality: officials valued silence more than the health of residents. The monetary trail linked the contractor's expansion payments to an office outside normal public oversight. Eli became moody when the new timetable reduced his planned private class hours. An independent core team became the nucleus of the island's new emergency communication network. A sharp rap at the metal door announced the safety inspector's arrival. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 紀錄揭露一種危險心態：官員重視安靜勝過居民健康。 金錢流向把承包商的擴建款項連到一個不受一般公共監督的辦公室。 新時刻表減少他預定的私人課程，言秋因此情緒不定。 一支獨立核心小組成為島上新緊急通訊網的核心。 金屬門上傳來急促敲擊聲，宣告安全檢查員抵達。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A red slash across the engineer's recommendation marked the moment the council ignored the warning. A recording captured a sleeping worker's snore before an illegal correction signal interrupted it. The telecommunications contract covered secure calls from trial stores. The chair agreed to unveil the contract terms before any final vote. An elderly boat captain defended the public path with surprising vigor, insisting that younger residents deserved safe access too. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 工程師建議上的紅色劃線，標記了議會無視警告的那一刻。 錄音收進一名熟睡工人的鼾聲，接著被違法修正訊號打斷。 電信合約涵蓋試辦門市的安全通訊。 主席同意在最終表決前公開契約條款。 一名年長船長充滿活力地捍衛公共步道，堅稱年輕居民也應享有安全通行。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Nara had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Eli wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Solven. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。奈拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。伊萊把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起索爾文總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. The corporation auctions an extra hour of winter daylight to the highest-bidding neighborhoods.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 公司把冬季多一小時日照拍賣給出價最高的街區。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Eli, a former mirror pilot, refuses to steer the array toward luxury towers while emergency shelters freeze.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 前反射鏡駕駛伊萊拒絕讓鏡陣朝向豪華大樓，任由避難所凍結。


## CHAPTER 06｜A Garden in a Basement｜地下室的花園

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：857**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Families cultivate vegetables using reclaimed heat and reflective foil in an abandoned transit station. Nara saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。家庭在廢棄車站用回收熱能與反光箔種蔬菜。奈拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Nara could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Eli suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，奈拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。伊萊提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The witness tapes survived because Eli's mother had stored them in airtight metal cylinders. The engineering team could not attain a safe restoration protocol without testing volunteers' reactions first. An old congresswoman had signed the emergency evacuation review and disappeared from later records. One sealed ration crate still contained curry packets labeled for the train station's night workers. Nara had begun to despise the lie, but she refused to treat every frightened employee as an enemy. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 證人錄音帶能留存，是因為言秋母親把它們裝在氣密金屬筒裡。 工程團隊若不先測試自願者反應，就無法達成安全的記憶恢復程序。 一名前任女性議員簽過緊急撤離審查，卻從後來紀錄中消失。 一箱密封口糧裡仍有咖哩包，標示是給車站夜班工人的。 奈拉開始厭惡謊言，卻拒絕把每位害怕的員工都視為敵人。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. Their harvest proves that company-controlled daylight is not the only source of survival. Eli asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Nara agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。他們的收成證明企業控制的日照不是生存的唯一來源。伊萊詢問是否可能只是出錯，奈拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The archived sky schedule described an eclipse that public lessons insisted had never occurred. Nari checked whether the old ration packs were still edible before placing them with the archive. Ivo's esteem for Nara grew when she chose to protect the witness names instead of seeking fame. A painted evergreen tree marked the maintenance tunnel that led away from the live network. A child asked whether a fish fin worked like the flap on the book box. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 封存的天空時刻表記載了一次日食，公共課程卻堅稱那從未發生。 娜莉把舊口糧一起封存前，先確認它們是否仍可食用。 奈拉選擇保護證人姓名而非追求名聲，讓伊沃對她更為尊重。 一棵彩繪常綠樹標記著通往現行網路之外的維修隧道。 孩子問魚鰭是否像書箱的活門那樣運作。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Nara insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Eli drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。奈拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。伊萊畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A flake of old paint fell from the ceiling when the evacuation tape began to play. The inventory distinguished emergency supplies from merchandise once sold to the missing district. The monotony of the relay's steady hum made its sudden silence in the chamber feel frightening. Keeping paper travel diaries had once been a common pastime before the Authority discouraged writing. The original design required redundancy: two independent archives in case one system failed or was compromised. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 撤離錄音帶開始播放時，一片老油漆從天花板剝落。 清冊將緊急物資與昔日出售給失蹤行政區的商品分開。 中繼器單調不變的嗡鳴，讓維修室裡突如其來的寂靜格外可怕。 在管理局不鼓勵書寫以前，紙本旅行日記曾是常見消遣。 原始設計要求備援機制：兩套獨立檔案，防止其中一套故障或遭滲透。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Eli wanted to roam the lower tunnels, but the court order authorized only one chamber. Vale had dismissed the engineers' warnings as rubbish without ever reading the measurements. An official poster promised to slay the 'monster of dreams,' turning ordinary sleep into an imagined enemy. A sly alteration in the tape label hid the passengers' true destination under a routine maintenance code. Cold vapor rose from the old cooling pipes as the preservation team opened the storage cabinet. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 言秋想在下層隧道四處探查，但法院命令只允許進入一間維修室。 維爾未曾讀過測量數據，就把工程師警告斥為垃圾話。 官方海報宣稱要殺死「夢的怪物」，把普通睡眠塑造成虛構敵人。 錄音帶標籤上的狡猾修改，把乘客真正目的地藏在例行維護代碼下。 保全小組打開儲存櫃時，舊冷卻管冒出冰冷水霧。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Nara passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Eli paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，奈拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。伊萊停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. Families cultivate vegetables using reclaimed heat and reflective foil in an abandoned transit station.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 家庭在廢棄車站用回收熱能與反光箔種蔬菜。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. Their harvest proves that company-controlled daylight is not the only source of survival.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 他們的收成證明企業控制的日照不是生存的唯一來源。


## CHAPTER 07｜The Pilot Who Quit｜辭職的駕駛

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：868**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Nara refused to begin with the official conclusion. Eli shares flight records showing light sent away from unpaid districts even during an energy surplus. In the ring-shadow city, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。奈拉拒絕從官方結論開始。伊萊提供飛行紀錄，顯示即使能源過剩，光線也被故意避開欠費街區。在環影城，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Nara asked Eli not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。奈拉請伊萊不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The bulky old tax ledgers could not fit through the school door without removing its damaged frame. The labor officer would deem required opening duties paid work regardless of their label. The elder called the city's memory policy a quiet dictatorship built from forms rather than soldiers. An unregistered delivery cart could glide along the lower rails without triggering street cameras. Children sold small handicraft birds to purchase paper that city printers refused to supply them. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 厚重的舊稅務帳冊，不拆掉損壞門框就搬不進學校。 勞工官會將營運必要工作認定為有薪勞動，不受名稱影響。 長者稱城市的記憶政策是一種靠表格而非士兵建立的無聲獨裁。 一輛未登記的送貨車能沿下層軌道滑行，不觸發街道攝影機。 孩子們販售手工小鳥，換取城市印刷廠拒絕供應的紙張。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. His license is suspended for revealing the data, leaving Nara responsible for safeguarding the originals. Nara watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Eli recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。他因洩露資料遭停照，原始檔案只能交由奈拉保管。奈拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。伊萊公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A community interpreter translated the inspectors' questions for elders who still used the old district dialect. The neighborhood's lesbian teacher said she feared losing her students, not the disclosure of her identity. For residents, liberation meant an address and protection from raids, not a speech from aboveground heroes. Nara rejected the lofty promise that everyone would be saved if they simply trusted her. Ivo used a paper utility map to navigate the tunnels without sending a digital location signal. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 社區口譯員替仍說舊區方言的長者翻譯檢查員的問題。 下層社區的女同志教師說，她害怕的是失去學生，而不是身分被揭露。 對居民而言，解放是擁有地址與免於突襲的保障，不是地面英雄的演說。 奈拉拒絕那種空泛崇高的承諾：只要信她，所有人就會得救。 伊沃用紙本管線圖穿越隧道，沒有發出數位定位訊號。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Solven demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Nara replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當索爾文總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。奈拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The lower district's volunteer medics warned that overwork would leave them unable to handle another raid. The elder placed a smooth pebble beside every confirmed name on the handwritten community register. The application for registered street addresses remained pending despite decades of tax payments. An abandoned plantation above the tunnels supplied seeds to gardens the city denied existed. The community used a hand plow to cultivate strips of soil beside the old service tracks. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 下層社區志工醫護警告，過勞會使他們無力應付下一次突襲。 長者在手寫社區名冊每個核實姓名旁放一顆光滑小石頭。 儘管居民繳了幾十年稅，街道地址的登記申請仍在待審。 隧道上方一座廢棄種植園提供種子，供給城市否認存在的菜園。 社區用手推犁在舊維修軌道旁耕作狹長土地。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: No member of any royalty had visited the lower district, though official portraits hung in its old school. The threat of another correction pulse could spur residents to hide rather than testify. Nara watched an elderly couple stroll down a street that did not appear on any official map. A boy with a deep tan from the garden lamps asked whether the real sun was warmer. The elder warned Nara not to treat the residents' missing citizenship as a trifle. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 沒有任何王室成員來過下層社區，舊學校卻還掛著官方肖像。 新一輪修正脈衝的威脅，可能促使居民躲藏而不是作證。 奈拉看見一對老夫婦漫步在任何官方地圖都沒有的街道。 一名被菜園燈曬黑的男孩問，真正的太陽會不會更暖。 長者警告奈拉，別把居民失去公民身分當成小事。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Nara resisted the urge to call it proof. Eli opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。奈拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。伊萊翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Eli shares flight records showing light sent away from unpaid districts even during an energy surplus.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 伊萊提供飛行紀錄，顯示即使能源過剩，光線也被故意避開欠費街區。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. His license is suspended for revealing the data, leaving Nara responsible for safeguarding the originals.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 他因洩露資料遭停照，原始檔案只能交由奈拉保管。


## CHAPTER 08｜The Children of the Roof｜屋頂上的孩子

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：855**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Schoolchildren build safe rooftop reflectors to share a small patch of morning light. Nara asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。學童在屋頂安全架設反光板，分享一小塊晨光。奈拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Nara listened longer than the schedule allowed. Eli reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。奈拉聽得比議程允許的更久。伊萊提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The hospital stocked every necessary antibiotic, but medicines could not reverse an improperly timed neural pulse. Families began to applaud when the court suspended the strongest correction settings in their district. An astronomer explained that the clinic's perfect sky was a projection, not evidence about the world outside. The Authority had tried to computerize every patient statement, leaving almost no independent paper copies. Ivo helped disable the overheating relay before it could injure anyone on the hospital floor. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 醫院備有必要的抗生素，藥物卻無法逆轉時機錯誤的神經脈衝。 法院暫停該區最強的修正設定時，家屬開始鼓掌。 一名天文學家解釋，診所完美的天空是投影，不能證明外面的世界如何。 管理局試圖將每份病患陳述電腦化，幾乎不留下獨立的紙本副本。 伊沃協助停用過熱中繼器，避免醫院樓層有人受傷。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. A company drone removes the devices, claiming ownership of every reflected beam. Nara read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Eli did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。公司無人機拆走裝置，聲稱每一道反射光都屬於企業。奈拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。伊萊沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Nurses had to evacuate patients according to the written plan rather than follow Vale's sudden instructions. The medical panel asked why expenditure on sleep control had tripled while ordinary clinics lost funding. One patient became irritable after a high-intensity pulse, a side effect hidden in the official statistics. The investigation asked whether the intermediary was Nara Shuo's kin. A teacher offered a quiet meditation room for anxious children, while medical staff kept the treatment area clear. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 護理師必須依書面計畫疏散病患，而非聽從維爾的臨時指示。 醫療小組詢問，為何睡眠控制支出增加三倍，一般診所預算卻遭削減。 一名病患接受高強度脈衝後變得易怒，這項副作用被官方統計隱藏。 調查要查明中間商是否為林朔親屬。 老師替焦慮的孩子安排安靜的冥想室，同時讓醫護治療區保持暢通。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Nara had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Eli proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 奈拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。伊萊提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A nickel coin fell from the cracked box of donated objects. The investigator used the floor map to orient new witnesses. The museum used the outdated label 'oriental' on a medical artifact; the curator agreed to replace it with a precise origin. A child treated for pneumonia had been wrongly classified as suffering from forbidden-dream sickness. The neurologist refused to activate the untested prototype until an ethics committee approved the trial. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一枚五分硬幣從破裂的捐贈物箱中掉出。 調查員用樓層圖協助新證人辨明方向。 博物館用過時的「東方的」標籤描述醫療文物，館長同意改用精確產地。 一名肺炎孩童被錯誤分類為罹患禁忌夢境疾病。 在倫理委員會批准試驗前，神經科醫師拒絕啟動未測試的原型機。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The psychiatry department confirmed that fear and loss of sleep could explain some patients' symptoms. The panel demanded a robust study with independent reviewers, not a promotional chart from the Authority. Vale claimed sovereign authority over all dreams, but the court questioned whether any such power existed. A hospital spokesperson said the ruling covered only one district and did not settle the wider case. Behind the children's wardrobe, staff found an unused cable channel leading to the relay maintenance shaft. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 精神醫學部確認，恐懼與睡眠不足能解釋部分病患症狀。 小組要求有獨立審查員參與的嚴謹研究，而非管理局的宣傳圖表。 維爾宣稱政府對所有夢境擁有主權，法院卻質疑這種權力是否存在。 醫院發言人表示，裁定只涵蓋一個行政區，尚未解決整體案件。 兒童衣櫃後面，員工發現一條未使用的電纜通道，通往中繼器維修井。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Nara returned to an unpaid sunlight meter. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Eli remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the ring-shadow city waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，奈拉再次看向一只欠費的陽光計量器。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。伊萊守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的環影城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Schoolchildren build safe rooftop reflectors to share a small patch of morning light.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 學童在屋頂安全架設反光板，分享一小塊晨光。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. A company drone removes the devices, claiming ownership of every reflected beam.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 公司無人機拆走裝置，聲稱每一道反射光都屬於企業。


## CHAPTER 09｜The Sunlight Clinic｜日照診所

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：853**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Doctors document preventable harm to patients who cannot pay for light-sensitive treatment schedules. Nara checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。醫師記錄付不起日照治療時段的病人遭受可避免傷害。奈拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** Eli arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Nara could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 伊萊帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。奈拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A teenager with acne said the clinic treated his ordinary discomfort as proof that dreams were dangerous. Only licensed staff could administer the sleep medication, yet automated records named absent employees. The Authority's brochure cited a medical study that no independent researcher could find. The children had been told that confinement protected them, but many described the clinic as captivity. Students were told not to cram for the lifeguard exam instead of practicing skills. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名長青春痘的少年說，診所把他的普通不適當成夢境有害的證據。 只有持照人員能施用安眠藥，系統紀錄卻列出當天缺席的員工。 管理局的宣傳手冊引用一項獨立研究人員誰也找不到的醫學研究。 孩子們被告知隔離是保護，許多人卻把診所形容為囚禁。 學生被提醒不要只臨時死背救生考試，而忽略練習技能。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. An emergency tribunal orders temporary medical access, but the company invoices the city for lost revenue. For a long moment no one spoke. Nara understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Eli broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。緊急法庭裁定暫時開放醫療日照，公司卻向市府索取營收損失。一時之間無人開口。奈拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。伊萊打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The instructor could detach the floating strap without requiring another person's assistance. One swimmer described mild discomfort after the new bench was installed. The forthcoming parent meeting would examine school transfer records rather than children's private dreams. The side windows helped illuminate the entrance without harsh overhead lighting. The school kept a small jade token donated by a parent from the missing eastern district. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 教練可以拆下浮具繫帶，不必仰賴他人協助。 一名泳者描述新長椅安裝後造成輕微不適。 即將舉行的家長會將檢查學校轉學紀錄，而不是孩子的私人夢境。 側窗有助照亮入口，又不必依賴刺眼頂燈。 學校保存著一枚小玉牌，是失蹤東區一名家長捐贈的。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Solven and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Nara rejected both. The issue was whether a private company may sell access to the natural sky, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住索爾文總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。奈拉兩者都拒絕。核心是私人企業是否有權出售人們接觸自然天空的機會，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Eli was joyous after entering the race, whatever result the timing board would show. The teacher taught media literacy by asking pupils how to tell a drawing from a verified historical record. The narrator of one memoir had deliberately hidden her own name. The smell of wet railway stone reached Nara's nostril when she opened a box of old school tiles. A map in the teacher's collection placed the missing railway on a peninsula outside the city wall. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 嘉寧報名比賽後滿心喜悅，不論計時板最後顯示什麼。 老師透過辨別圖畫與經驗證史料的差異，教授媒體識讀能力。 一部回憶錄的敘述者刻意隱去自己的名字。 奈拉打開一箱舊學校地磚，濕潤鐵路石頭的氣味鑽進她鼻孔。 老師收藏的地圖把失蹤鐵路畫在城牆外一座半島上。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The potent combination of censorship and repeated correction had distorted what many residents thought possible. The revival of local history did not require exposing private notebooks. The city promised a selective audit of contracts with the highest financial risk. One parent searched for a stepchild whose name appeared on the same vanished train list. A child's picture of a trout beneath the railway bridge matched a fishing guide printed before the founding. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 審查與反覆修正的強大組合，扭曲了許多居民對可能性的認知。 地方史的復興不必以公開私人筆記為代價。 市府承諾針對財務風險最高的合約進行選擇性稽核。 一名家長尋找自己的繼子女，名字出現在同一份消失列車名單上。 孩子畫在鐵路橋下的鱒魚，與建城以前出版的釣魚指南吻合。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Nara had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Eli wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Solven. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。奈拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。伊萊把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起索爾文總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. Doctors document preventable harm to patients who cannot pay for light-sensitive treatment schedules.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 醫師記錄付不起日照治療時段的病人遭受可避免傷害。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. An emergency tribunal orders temporary medical access, but the company invoices the city for lost revenue.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 緊急法庭裁定暫時開放醫療日照，公司卻向市府索取營收損失。


## CHAPTER 10｜The Scarcity Was Invented｜短缺是被製造的

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：863**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Nara decodes the orbital logs and shows that most light was diverted into an unused military mirror reserve. Nara knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。奈拉解開軌道日誌，證明多數陽光被轉送到閒置的軍用鏡陣儲備。奈拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Nara spread the orbital allocation log across three screens, then covered the company's billing figures with a sheet of paper. The physical light measurements told a simpler story. Every dark district had been denied a share of sunlight that the mirrors were already reflecting into a sealed military reserve. The reserve was almost full. Solven had called the shortage natural and raised prices each time a neighborhood protested. Eli placed the original pilot orders beside the chart. Their signatures were authentic; the justification was not. When the public hearing began, Nara asked the director one question: 'If there is no sunlight left to sell, why are your military mirrors throwing it into empty space?' The broadcast cut to black before he answered.

**繁中** 奈拉把軌道分配日誌鋪滿三面螢幕，再用一張紙遮住公司帳單數字。真實的光量測數據顯示更簡單的故事：每個黑暗街區被拒絕分配的陽光，早已被鏡陣反射進封閉的軍用儲備，幾乎滿載。索爾文稱短缺是自然現象，街區每次抗議便加價。伊萊把原始駕駛命令放在圖表旁。簽名是真的，理由卻不是。公開聽證開始時，奈拉只問總監一件事：「如果沒有陽光可賣，為什麼你們的軍用鏡子正把它拋進空無一物的太空？」他還沒回答，直播便轉成黑畫面。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An old training film used an animate cartoon train to teach children the route beyond the western gate. Simple arithmetic showed that reported train repairs continued long after the official closure date. Ivo called the live coastal signal an awesome discovery, then carefully noted its unverified source. The railway worker offered celery soup made from vegetables grown in a garden near the sealed gate. Fresh cement around the old gate contradicted claims that no one had maintained it for decades. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一部舊教學影片用栩栩如生的卡通列車教孩子認識西門外路線。 簡單的算術就能看出，列車維修紀錄在官方封閉日期之後仍持續多年。 伊沃說即時海岸訊號是驚人的發現，隨後仍謹慎註明來源尚未驗證。 鐵路維修員拿出芹菜湯，食材種在封閉城門旁的菜園。 舊城門周圍的新水泥，與數十年無人維護的說法矛盾。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Solven admits the subscription price was based on artificial scarcity rather than physical shortage. Nara felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。索爾文承認訂閱價格依據人為短缺，而非真正的物理不足。奈拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The coastal radio mentioned a confederation of towns that the city's textbooks described as extinct. A public crackdown on unauthorized boats could not substitute for fixing confusing instructions, Eli argued at the hearing. A crocodile drawing on the children's wall kept the safety lesson playful. The old railway had a dual power system, allowing trains to operate even when city electricity failed. The city could not equalize every skill level, but it could make entry fairer. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 海岸廣播提到一個城鎮邦聯，城市教科書卻稱其早已消亡。 言秋在聽證時主張，嚴厲取締未經許可的船隻不能取代修正令人困惑的指示。 兒童牆上的鱷魚圖讓安全課帶點趣味。 舊鐵路有雙重電力系統，即使城市停電，列車仍能運行。 市府無法讓每個人的技能相同，卻能使入場機會更公平。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Nara insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Eli drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。奈拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。伊萊畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A council faction argued that opening the gate would endanger the city's fragile social order. The gate engineer feared he would incur criminal penalties merely for preserving an obsolete switch. Residents called unequal access to safety information an injustice, especially when only wealthy boat owners received private warnings. The unexplained coastal signal deepened the intrigue surrounding the city's false founding history. A flashy app could lure officials away from the needs of paper-card residents. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一個議會派系主張開城門會危及城市脆弱的社會秩序。 城門工程師害怕只因保存淘汰轉轍器，就會受到刑事處罰。 居民認為安全資訊分配不均是一種不公，尤其只有富有船主能得到私人警示。 無法解釋的海岸訊號，讓城市虛假建城史背後的陰謀更加撲朔迷離。 華麗應用程式可能引誘官員忽略紙本求援急救手冊證居民。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Vale called the inspector obstinate for refusing to surrender the old receiver without a warrant. A pickpocket had once stolen a train ticket and kept it as a souvenir after the railway disappeared. The cleaning crew left on time because the new contracts required punctual shifts. The chair showed restraint by allowing critics to finish their comments. After twelve hours of testimony, the exhausted witness began to stagger toward the exit, and the chair called a break. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 維爾說檢查員固執，只因他拒絕在沒有令狀時交出舊接收器。 一名扒手曾偷過火車票，鐵路消失後卻把票當作紀念品保存。 清潔團隊準時下班，因為新合約要求準點輪班。 主席克制自己，讓批評者把話說完。 證人作證十二小時後，疲憊得往出口踉蹌，主席因此宣布休息。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Nara passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Eli paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，奈拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。伊萊停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。
