### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Nara decodes the orbital logs and shows that most light was diverted into an unused military mirror reserve.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 奈拉解開軌道日誌，證明多數陽光被轉送到閒置的軍用鏡陣儲備。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Solven admits the subscription price was based on artificial scarcity rather than physical shortage.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 索爾文承認訂閱價格依據人為短缺，而非真正的物理不足。


## CHAPTER 11｜The Terms of Daylight｜日照服務條款

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：836**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. The corporation argues that citizens agreed to payment by opening their smart windows. Nara had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the ring-shadow city, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。公司主張公民打開智慧窗戶就等於同意付費。奈拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在環影城，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Nara could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Eli suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，奈拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。伊萊提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Vale sought public acclaim for the dream ban, but the authenticated invoices weakened his heroic image. The shared-account policy contained an ambiguity that allowed several technicians to use one password without recording their names. The report ignored bodily limits that had been stated in the original medical note. A cold draught crossed the hearing room when someone opened the ventilation hatch. The archived machine log measured heat in Fahrenheit, unlike the city's present metric settings. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 維爾希望禁夢政策帶來公眾讚譽，認證發票卻動搖他的英雄形象。 共用帳號政策有一處模糊之處，容許多名技師共用密碼而不記姓名。 報告忽略原始醫療紀錄中已明列的身體限制。 有人打開通風口時，一股冷風穿過聽證室。 封存機械紀錄用華氏溫度量測熱能，與現行公制設定不同。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. Nara proves no household received a meaningful option to refuse the new terms. Eli asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Nara agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。奈拉證明所有家庭都沒有真正拒絕新條款的選項。伊萊詢問是否可能只是出錯，奈拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The original train invoices survived inside a fireproof document case beneath the market counter. A geographical discrepancy placed the old station west of a wall the official map said had always existed. Nara refused to call an anonymous hacker as her only witness when the documents could be verified lawfully. Nari explained the relay mechanism in terms a layman could understand without sacrificing accuracy. A court could liberate the records from secrecy, but it could not decide what every resident must remember. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 原始列車發票存放在市場櫃檯下的防火文件箱，因此得以倖存。 地理上的矛盾，把舊車站定位在官方宣稱一直存在的城牆西側。 文件能依法核對時，奈拉拒絕把匿名駭客當成唯一證人。 娜莉用外行人能理解、又不失準確的方式解釋中繼器機制。 法院可以讓紀錄擺脫保密，卻不能決定每位居民必須記得什麼。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Solven demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Nara replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當索爾文總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。奈拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A mermaid mural from the lost railway appeared on two photographs taken decades apart. The seized archive included miscellaneous receipts that proved surprisingly important to the timeline. The committee recorded every occurrence of unauthorized contract changes. A family outing ticket listed a train route that the government had later erased from transit maps. The staff promised to persevere through the difficult budget review. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 失蹤鐵路上的美人魚壁畫，出現在相隔幾十年拍攝的兩張照片裡。 查扣檔案裡有各種雜項收據，對重建時間線竟出乎意料重要。 委員會記錄每次未授權修改合約的事件。 一張家庭出遊車票印有後來遭政府從運輸圖抹除的列車路線。 員工承諾堅持完成艱難的預算審查。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The judge asked the court to ponder how a government could collect taxes from an officially nonexistent district. The hearing began to revolve around verifiable records instead of arguments about whose dreams felt convincing. Eli put a clean utensil beside the tea, keeping food away from documents. Eli warned that labeling Eli a villain before the inquiry ended would not replace evidence. A market yogurt container carried a printed delivery address from the supposedly destroyed station district. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 法官請法庭思考：政府怎能向官方不存在的行政區收稅？ 聽證逐漸圍繞可查的紀錄，而不是爭論誰的夢感覺更真實。 宋穎把乾淨餐具放在茶旁，讓食物遠離文件。 言秋提醒，調查尚未結束前先把言秋叫做惡人，不能取代證據。 一個市場優格容器印著據稱已毀車站區的送貨地址。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Nara resisted the urge to call it proof. Eli opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。奈拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。伊萊翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. The corporation argues that citizens agreed to payment by opening their smart windows.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 公司主張公民打開智慧窗戶就等於同意付費。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Nara proves no household received a meaningful option to refuse the new terms.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 奈拉證明所有家庭都沒有真正拒絕新條款的選項。


## CHAPTER 12｜The Dark Districts March｜黑暗街區遊行

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：856**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Residents from twenty dark districts carry mirrors to the public square without attacking company workers. Nara saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。二十個黑暗街區的居民帶著鏡子走上廣場，沒有攻擊公司員工。奈拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Nara asked Eli not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。奈拉請伊萊不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A laboratory attendant identified the storage shelves where the children's original treatment registers had been kept. The cooking class showed how batter could stain a cotton apron. The old census counted hundreds of children absent from the city's approved founding population. A retired chemist explained how old ink could be dated without damaging the children's records. Birds began to chirp outside the hall as the budget workshop ended. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名實驗室服務員指出，兒童原始治療名冊存放在哪些架上。 烹飪課示範麵糊如何弄髒棉圍裙。 舊人口普查列出數百名不在官方建城人口名單上的兒童。 退休化學家解釋如何不損傷兒童紀錄，就能測定舊墨水年代。 預算工作坊結束時，鳥兒開始在禮堂外啁啾。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Their peaceful light display reveals how much sunlight still reaches the city from the edges of the array. Nara watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Eli recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。和平反光展示揭露鏡陣邊緣仍有大量陽光抵達城市。奈拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。伊萊公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The clinic agreed to dispense basic medical supplies during the night trial but kept emergency medication under professional supervision. The technician let out a groan when he discovered that the latest digital copy lacked entire pages. The missing laboratory files were only the visible tip of an iceberg of altered civic records. The undamaged consent forms proved invaluable for distinguishing voluntary treatment from compulsory correction. Eli had funded a lavish celebration of safe sleep while cutting staff assigned to monitor side effects. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 第二只時鐘又顯示矛盾時間戳時，疲倦的工程師輕輕呻吟。 技師發現最新數位副本缺了整頁時，發出一聲呻吟。 失蹤的實驗室檔案，只是整批遭修改市政紀錄露出的冰山一角。 完好的同意書對區分自願治療與強制修正極其珍貴。 言秋砸錢舉辦奢華安眠慶典，卻削減追蹤副作用的人力。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Nara had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Eli proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 奈拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。伊萊提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The original clinic logo showed an open hand, unlike the lifespan exchange's later symbol of a watchful eye. A loudspeaker ordered visitors to leave, but the judge's preservation warrant kept the examination team inside. New martial security regulations threatened to transfer the medical archive to an armed enforcement office. No confirmed outbreak preceded the children's transfers, despite what the founding textbooks claimed. A preserved oyster shell in Nara's childhood box suggested a visit to the coast before the city sealed its gates. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 原診所標誌是一隻張開的手，不同於壽命交易所後來監視的眼睛符號。 擴音器命令訪客離開，但法官的保全令使檢查小組得以留下。 新的軍事化安全規章威脅要把醫療檔案移交武裝執法辦公室。 儘管建城教科書如此宣稱，兒童轉移之前沒有任何確診疫情爆發。 梅童年盒子裡保存的牡蠣殼，顯示城門封鎖前她可能去過海岸。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The early engineers never intended the technology to populate every home with compulsory monitors. The fallen tree was a setback, not a reason to abandon the service. Some residents remained skeptical of the proposed licensed detour. Visitors could subscribe to updates without buying the collection. The charity promised not to withhold apprentice wages during an audit. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 早期工程師從未打算讓強制監測環遍布每戶家庭。 倒木是挫折，不是放棄服務的理由。 部分居民仍懷疑提議中的合格繞道路線。 來賓不必購買系列，也能訂閱更新資訊。 慈善機構承諾稽核期間不扣留學徒薪水。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Nara returned to an unpaid sunlight meter. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Eli remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the ring-shadow city waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，奈拉再次看向一只欠費的陽光計量器。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。伊萊守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的環影城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. Residents from twenty dark districts carry mirrors to the public square without attacking company workers.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 二十個黑暗街區的居民帶著鏡子走上廣場，沒有攻擊公司員工。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. Their peaceful light display reveals how much sunlight still reaches the city from the edges of the array.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 和平反光展示揭露鏡陣邊緣仍有大量陽光抵達城市。


## CHAPTER 13｜A Contract Written in Orbit｜寫在軌道上的契約

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：845**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Nara refused to begin with the official conclusion. Eli retrieves the original orbital construction charter from a retired satellite station. In the ring-shadow city, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。奈拉拒絕從官方結論開始。伊萊從退役衛星站取回軌道工程原始章程。在環影城，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Nara listened longer than the schedule allowed. Eli reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。奈拉聽得比議程允許的更久。伊萊提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The central contention at the meeting was whether truth justified forcing people to relive trauma. A former laboratory worker described how familiar cosmetics scents had been used as sensory anchors. An elderly woman held a plain cracker to remember the taste of meals from the missing district. A licensed electrician showed the council how local relays could be isolated without cutting hospital power. A tear clung to the mother's eyelash as she described forgetting her son's laughter. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 會議的核心爭論是：真相是否能正當化強迫人們重歷創傷。 一名前實驗室員工描述，熟悉的化妝品香氣曾被用作感官錨點。 一名老婦人握著一片原味餅乾，想記住失蹤行政區餐點的味道。 持照電工向議會展示如何不切斷醫院電力就隔離地方中繼器。 母親說起忘掉兒子的笑聲時，一滴淚停在她的睫毛上。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. It classifies the array as public infrastructure leased for maintenance, not a product that can be owned forever. Nara read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Eli did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。章程將鏡陣定為供維護使用的公共設施，而不是可永久擁有的商品。奈拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。伊萊沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The campaign's glamour could not hide weak account security. Nara refused to humiliate citizens who still believed the Authority's medical claims. The imminent synchronization event gave the legal panel only days to act. The smell of the old orchard could linger in a person's mind despite years of correction signals. One resident described lost livestock records as proof that farming communities had existed beyond the wall. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 宣傳的華麗掩蓋不了帳號安全漏洞。 奈拉拒絕羞辱仍相信管理局醫療說法的市民。 即將到來的同步活動，讓法律小組只剩幾天能採取行動。 舊果園的氣味可能在人的心裡徘徊，即使多年遭到修正訊號干擾。 一名居民提到失蹤的牲畜紀錄，證明城牆外曾有農業社區。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Solven and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Nara rejected both. The issue was whether a private company may sell access to the natural sky, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住索爾文總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。奈拉兩者都拒絕。核心是私人企業是否有權出售人們接觸自然天空的機會，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The old screen could magnify the results for people seated at the back. The proposed safeguard was not reliant on a single network administrator or political leader. The sanitation team checked shower areas between the morning and afternoon sessions. The council advertised scenic views from the dome while hiding the living neighborhoods below it. The emergency plan required engineers to open each relay cabinet's mechanical shutter before isolation. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 舊螢幕可放大成績，讓後排坐著的人看清楚。 提議的防護措施不依賴單一網路管理員或政治領袖。 衛生團隊在早、午場之間檢查淋浴區。 議會宣傳穹頂的美麗風景，卻藏起下方仍有人居住的社區。 緊急計畫要求工程師在隔離前打開每個中繼櫃的機械百葉門。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The inspector waited for the temporary platform to stabilize before approving entry. Vale's stylish presentation could not answer the question of whether citizens had consented. The coalition offered to supplement its medical petition with authenticated records from affected families. The coalition requested suspension of the strongest pulses, not compulsory erasure of existing memories. The speaker slowed her tempo when she explained that recovering history must respect individual choice. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 檢查員等臨時平台穩定後，才准許入場。 維爾時髦精美的簡報，仍答不出市民是否同意的問題。 聯盟願意以受影響家庭的認證紀錄補充醫療請願。 聯盟要求暫停最強脈衝，而不是強制抹除既有記憶。 發言者解釋恢復歷史必須尊重個人選擇時，放慢了語速。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Nara had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Eli wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Solven. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。奈拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。伊萊把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起索爾文總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Eli retrieves the original orbital construction charter from a retired satellite station.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 伊萊從退役衛星站取回軌道工程原始章程。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. It classifies the array as public infrastructure leased for maintenance, not a product that can be owned forever.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 章程將鏡陣定為供維護使用的公共設施，而不是可永久擁有的商品。


## CHAPTER 14｜The Engineer Who Signed｜曾簽名的工程師

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：851**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Nara discovers her own mentor approved an early billing prototype without anticipating mass disconnection. Nara asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。奈拉發現自己的恩師曾核准早期計費原型，卻沒想到會造成大規模斷光。奈拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** Eli arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Nara could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 伊萊帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。奈拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Local adaptation of the shutdown procedure was required because each neighborhood used different relay hardware. Ira added a steel brace to the stair railing while the trustees debated a longer-term restoration plan. Nari opposed a charitable publicity event that offered food only to residents willing to endorse Vale. It had become customary for citizens to accept overnight corrections without reading the consent notices. The museum's modest endowment was enough for document storage, not for rebuilding the entire viewing platform. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 由於各社區中繼硬體不同，停機程序需要因地調整。 受託人討論長期修復方案時，伊拉先替樓梯扶手加裝鋼支架。 娜莉反對一場以支持維爾換取食物的慈善宣傳活動。 市民已習慣不讀同意通知就接受每晚修正。 博物館有限的捐贈基金足夠保存文件，卻無法重建整座觀景平台。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. The mentor admits responsibility and offers a safer migration plan rather than asking for forgiveness. For a long moment no one spoke. Nara understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Eli broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。恩師承擔責任，提出安全遷移方案，而非乞求原諒。一時之間無人開口。奈拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。伊萊打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: One parent called the flood a freak event, but records showed similar warnings before. The old freeway beneath the dome had become a corridor for cables that nobody had audited. Each inhabitant of the trial district received a written explanation of the relay isolation. The removal of the strongest pulses seemed to lighten some patients' headaches, though more study was needed. The founding propaganda described the dome's majesty as proof that the authorities deserved unquestioned trust. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一名家長稱淹水是怪異偶發事件，紀錄卻顯示過去也有類似警告。 穹頂下的舊高速公路成了無人稽核的電纜走廊。 試行區每位居民都收到地方中繼隔離的書面說明。 移除最強脈衝似乎減輕了部分病患頭痛，但仍需更多研究。 建城宣傳把穹頂的宏偉當成當局值得無條件信任的證明。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Nara insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Eli drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。奈拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。伊萊畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The dirty overflow could mar the freshly painted wall but not the evidence log. The company tried to mimic an official seal on its promotional page. The city's modernization budget hid maintenance costs for the compulsory correction network. A mournful song drifted from the lower neighborhood after residents heard the missing children's testimony. A puff of dust escaped the relay cabinet when its long-sealed door was finally opened. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 言秋缺乏必要數據時，不願假裝模仿專家的判斷。 公司試圖在宣傳頁模仿官方印章。 城市現代化預算隱藏了強制修正網路的維護成本。 居民聽見失蹤兒童證詞後，下層社區傳來哀傷的歌聲。 長年密封的中繼櫃終於打開時，一小團灰塵飄了出來。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Nara's reflective notes admitted uncertainty instead of presenting her old patch as harmless. The nurses offered refreshment to residents waiting for the overnight medical observations. An obsolete relay panel carried a reptile-shaped mark matching the children's alligator poster. Nara needed an hour of solitude before she could face the review of her own engineering work. Vale arranged a succession of loyal deputies to defend the network if the council suspended him. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 奈拉的反思筆記承認不確定性，沒有把舊修補程式說成無害。 護理師向等待夜間醫療觀察的居民提供茶點。 淘汰中繼面板上有一個爬蟲形標記，與孩子們的鱷魚海報吻合。 面對自己工程成果的審查前，奈拉需要一小時獨處。 維爾安排一連串忠誠副手，準備在議會停職他時繼續捍衛網路。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Nara passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Eli paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，奈拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。伊萊停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Nara discovers her own mentor approved an early billing prototype without anticipating mass disconnection.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 奈拉發現自己的恩師曾核准早期計費原型，卻沒想到會造成大規模斷光。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The mentor admits responsibility and offers a safer migration plan rather than asking for forgiveness.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 恩師承擔責任，提出安全遷移方案，而非乞求原諒。


## CHAPTER 15｜The Heat Wave｜熱浪

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：846**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. A sudden heat wave makes unrestricted sunlight dangerous, complicating calls to destroy the orbital shade immediately. Nara checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。突發熱浪使無限制日照也變得危險，立即摧毀軌道遮光層的呼聲面臨難題。奈拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Nara asked Eli to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。奈拉請伊萊說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A senior official smoked a cigar while claiming the archived safety warnings had never been serious. The panel said personal closure could not replace compensation or corrective action for harmed patients. The mapped descent to the hidden archive began beneath the lower district's abandoned pumping station. The Authority had tried to dispose of obsolete consoles before investigators could copy their physical logs. Nari proposed to diversify the oversight committee so residents, doctors, and engineers all had votes. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名高階官員抽著雪茄，聲稱封存的安全警告從未值得重視。 小組指出，個人心理上的釋懷不能取代對受害病患的補償與改善措施。 地圖標示通往隱藏檔案的下行通道，起點在下層社區廢棄抽水站下方。 管理局曾企圖丟棄淘汰控制台，搶在調查員複製實體紀錄以前。 娜莉提議讓監督委員會成員更多元，使居民、醫師與工程師都有投票權。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Nara proposes public control with temperature safeguards instead of a dramatic but reckless shutdown. Nara felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。奈拉提議在溫度保護下實施公共控制，而非戲劇化卻魯莽的停機。奈拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Eli had learned to excel at software optimization yet failed to question the institutions deploying her work. Her fascination with engineering had once kept her from noticing whose interests the system served. Vale became a political foe, but Nara refused to make hatred of him the foundation of reform. The bay began to glitter beneath evening clouds, even while workers checked the event's dangerous cables. The city's official astronomy model depicted only one hemisphere, concealing observations from beyond the wall. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 言秋擅長軟體最佳化，卻沒質疑使用她作品的機構。 她對工程的著迷，曾使她忽略系統究竟服務誰的利益。 維爾成為政治對手，奈拉卻拒絕以仇恨他作為改革基礎。 傍晚雲層下海灣閃著光，工人卻仍在檢查活動中的危險電線。 城市官方天文模型只呈現一個半球，隱藏了城牆外的觀測。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Solven demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Nara replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當索爾文總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。奈拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Innumerable small edits had accumulated across the population, making restoration a medical as well as legal challenge. Nara's technical mastery did not excuse her failure to demand independent deployment tests. The notorious correction program had been presented as a harmless treatment for disordered sleep. The panel rejected outright the claim that Eli's negligence made all other documents false. The state postponed the premiere of its promotional film until after the independent hearing. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 無數細微修改在全體居民身上累積，使恢復同時成為醫療與法律難題。 奈拉的技術專精，不能替她未要求獨立部署測試開脫。 惡名昭彰的修正計畫，起初被包裝成治療睡眠混亂的無害方法。 小組斷然駁回「言秋疏失使其他文件全屬虛假」的說法。 國家把宣傳影片首映延到獨立聽證之後。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Vale tried to seduce skeptical officials with promises of stability and renewed funding. A sealed report mentioned a slaughter during the old evacuation, but investigators needed corroboration before assigning responsibility. The nutrition program focused on preventing food insecurity rather than romanticizing starvation. One retired statesman agreed to authenticate the old council signatures without claiming to remember every meeting. The tedious comparison of approval logs revealed exactly when Eli's patch had been expanded without consent. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 維爾試圖以穩定與新預算承諾誘惑持懷疑態度的官員。 一份密封報告提到舊撤離時的屠殺，但調查員仍需交叉證據才能判定責任。 一名退休政治家以居民身分旁聽，沒有要求特權。 一名退休政治家同意鑑定舊議會簽名，卻不宣稱記得每場會議。 繁瑣比對核准紀錄，揭露言秋的修補程式何時在未經同意下被擴張。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Nara resisted the urge to call it proof. Eli opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。奈拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。伊萊翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

## CHAPTER 16｜The Shareholders' Winter｜股東的冬季

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：859**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Investors demand higher profits despite a winter forecast that places unpaid neighborhoods at risk. Nara knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。即使冬季預報顯示欠費街區有危險，投資人仍要求提高獲利。奈拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Nara could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Eli suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，奈拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。伊萊提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Dust had begun to accumulate around the empty cabinet where a heavy archive box had recently stood. Beginners needed time to accustom themselves to the temperature and sound of the room. Eli recognized an affectionate note from her mother beside the original school roster. The old astronomy passage survived because it had been omitted from the modern relay network. Ira limited caffeine during the long watch and scheduled breaks, knowing alert workers also needed sleep. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一只大檔案箱不久前還放著的空櫃周圍，灰塵開始堆積。 初學者需要時間習慣館內溫度與聲響。 言秋在原始學校名冊旁認出母親寫的一句充滿愛意的話。 舊天文通道得以保存，因為它未接入現代中繼網路。 伊拉在漫長值班時控制咖啡因攝取並安排休息，知道警覺的工人也需要睡眠。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. A pension trustee refuses to support continued billing once independent injury figures become public. Eli asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Nara agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。退休基金受託人看到獨立傷害數據後，拒絕繼續支持收費。伊萊詢問是否可能只是出錯，奈拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Investigators found a clone of the official map in which deleted towns were visible under older names. A student contestant in the school history fair had once drawn the same vanished southern railway. The map was not definitive proof of deportation, but it gave investigators verifiable locations to inspect. Workers used a specified mild detergent on the casing, avoiding chemicals that might damage the sensor seals. The archive volunteers needed endurance, not dramatic courage, to compare thousands of handwritten entries. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 調查員找到一份官方地圖複製版，被刪城鎮在上面仍以舊名出現。 一名參加學校歷史展的學生選手，曾畫過同一條消失的南方鐵路。 地圖不能成為強制遷移的最終證明，卻提供了可實地查驗的地點。 工人用指定的溫和清潔劑清理外殼，避免化學物質傷害感測器密封。 檔案志工比對數千筆手寫紀錄，靠的是耐力而非戲劇化勇氣。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Nara had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Eli proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 奈拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。伊萊提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The structural engineer used a mechanical gauge to test pressure before opening the old hatch. An old photograph of a school goalkeeper helped date a class roster from the missing district. Council members wanted to modernize the archive, but residents insisted that paper originals remain protected. A child's lunch note mentioning pickled radish matched a recipe preserved by the lower district elder. Nara's realization that someone had entered the hidden archive recently changed the team's security plan. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 結構工程師在打開舊艙門前，用機械儀表測試壓力。 一張學校守門員的舊照片，協助判定失蹤行政區班級名冊的年代。 議員希望讓檔案現代化，居民卻堅持保護紙本正本。 一張提到醃蘿蔔的兒童午餐字條，與下層社區長者保存的食譜吻合。 奈拉意識到最近有人進入隱藏檔案庫，這改變了小組安全計畫。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: An old textbook blamed socialism for vanished towns, though its political claims lacked supporting documents. Personal recognition was subjective, so the surveyor checked every landmark against independent measurements. Eli could sympathize with workers facing layoffs while still requiring the operator to disclose the failed sensor. The old school medical ward appeared on the map despite being absent from the current city registry. Eli felt weary after the meeting and chose to rest rather than drive. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一本舊課本把城鎮消失歸咎於社會主義，政治說法卻缺乏文件支持。 個人認得的感覺很主觀，因此測量員用獨立數據核對每處地標。 言秋能同情面臨裁員的工人，仍要求營運商揭露感測器失效。 舊學校醫療病房出現在地圖上，現行城市戶籍卻沒有它。 會後言秋感到疲倦，選擇休息而非開車。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Nara returned to an unpaid sunlight meter. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Eli remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the ring-shadow city waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，奈拉再次看向一只欠費的陽光計量器。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。伊萊守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的環影城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Investors demand higher profits despite a winter forecast that places unpaid neighborhoods at risk.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 即使冬季預報顯示欠費街區有危險，投資人仍要求提高獲利。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. A pension trustee refuses to support continued billing once independent injury figures become public.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 退休基金受託人看到獨立傷害數據後，拒絕繼續支持收費。


## CHAPTER 17｜The Mirror Pilot's Test｜鏡陣駕駛測試

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：847**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Eli and Nara conduct a supervised trial that redirects a modest amount of light without damaging the array. Nara had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the ring-shadow city, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。伊萊與奈拉在監督下進行試驗，少量改變光線方向且不損壞鏡陣。奈拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在環影城，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Nara asked Eli not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。奈拉請伊萊不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An abstraction about fairness became useful only when it changed an actual schedule. Medical teams checked each patient's airway before attributing any symptoms to the correction pulse. A speaker could amplify the announcement but could not repair unclear wording. A sealed data capsule held the unauthorized test pulse log for independent examination. The curfew barred ordinary travel after midnight but did not cancel court-authorized safety inspections. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 關於公平的抽象概念，必須真的改變時刻表才有用。 醫療團隊先檢查每名病患的呼吸道，再判斷症狀是否與修正脈衝有關。 擴音器可以放大公告聲音，卻無法修補不清楚的用字。 一個密封資料膠囊保存未授權測試脈衝紀錄，以供獨立查核。 宵禁禁止一般居民午夜後外出，卻沒有取消法院授權的安全檢查。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. The test proves neighborhoods can receive basic daylight under a transparent public schedule. Nara watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Eli recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。測試證明街區能依公開時間表取得基本日照。奈拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。伊萊公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The residents' peaceful defiance consisted of keeping written records despite the lifespan exchange's warnings. A contractor received a dividend while the city reduced funding for the clinics harmed by its network. Ivo used the tunnel geometry to explain why certain relays could be isolated without cutting power. Shelter volunteers divided grapefruit among patients waiting for evaluation after the overnight pulse. The contractor wanted to harden the coastal track with concrete, but ecologists asked how runoff would affect the cliffs. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 居民的和平反抗，是即使壽命交易所警告，仍繼續保存書面紀錄。 一家承包商領了股利，城市卻削減受到網路傷害的診所預算。 伊沃用隧道幾何形狀解釋，為何能隔離某些中繼器而不斷電。 避難所志工把葡萄柚分給等待夜間脈衝評估的病患。 承包商想用混凝土硬化海岸道路，生態學家則詢問逕流對懸崖的影響。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Solven and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Nara rejected both. The issue was whether a private company may sell access to the natural sky, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住索爾文總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。奈拉兩者都拒絕。核心是私人企業是否有權出售人們接觸自然天空的機會，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: An honorary council title did not give Vale the right to ignore a judicial injunction. One historical costume depicted a heroine trying to imprison a corrupt ruler. One notable detail in the relay log was the account number used for the unauthorized test. A local playwright drafted a public performance about the history of the sealed lower streets. A quiet procession of neighbors accompanied the affected families to the public counter to submit their objections. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 榮譽議會頭銜不賦予維爾無視司法禁令的權利。 一件歷史戲服描繪女英雄逃離監禁。 中繼紀錄有一項值得注意的細節：未授權測試使用的帳戶號碼。 一名地方劇作家寫了關於封閉下層街道歷史的公開表演。 鄰居們組成安靜的隊伍，陪同受影響家庭到公共櫃台提交異議。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The serial number on the opened relay cabinet matched the contractor's maintenance form. A sociology student interviewed workers about the meaning of authorship. Ivo tried to soothe frightened residents by explaining what the court order actually protected. Reporters began to swarm the courthouse once the unauthorized test became public. The control building's false dawn cast an upward beam across the closed windows. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 被打開的中繼櫃序號與承包商維修表吻合。 社會學學生訪談工人對創作者署名的看法。 伊沃解釋法院命令實際保障的內容，試圖安撫害怕的居民。 未授權測試曝光後，記者開始湧向法院。 控制大樓的人造黎明向上投射一道光，掠過緊閉窗戶。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Nara had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Eli wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Solven. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。奈拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。伊萊把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起索爾文總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Eli and Nara conduct a supervised trial that redirects a modest amount of light without damaging the array.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 伊萊與奈拉在監督下進行試驗，少量改變光線方向且不損壞鏡陣。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The test proves neighborhoods can receive basic daylight under a transparent public schedule.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 測試證明街區能依公開時間表取得基本日照。


## CHAPTER 18｜The Executive's Forecast｜高層的預報

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：841**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Solven predicts chaos if the company loses exclusive control, and distributes a simulated blackout video. Nara saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。索爾文預言企業失去獨占後會混亂，散播模擬停電影片。奈拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Nara listened longer than the schedule allowed. Eli reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。奈拉聽得比議程允許的更久。伊萊提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An old astronaut training file listed external settlements, contradicting the claim that all humanity lived in Everday. The political breakup of the first Memory Commission left records scattered across several departments. A senior bureaucrat had signed deportation orders while describing them as routine public-health transfers. The auditor used a mechanical calculator to verify the relocation payments independently of digital records. The children in an old photograph looked carefree before officials moved their families without explanation. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一份舊太空人訓練檔案列出外部聚落，與全人類只住在永晝的說法矛盾。 首屆記憶委員會的政治解體，使紀錄散落各部門。 一名高階官僚簽署強制遷移命令，卻把它說成例行公共衛生轉移。 稽核員用機械計算機核對遷移款項，不依賴數位紀錄。 舊照片中的孩子看起來無憂無慮，後來官員卻毫無解釋地遷走家庭。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Nara finds the simulation omitted functioning municipal backup systems. Nara read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Eli did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。奈拉查出模擬刻意排除仍在運作的市政備援系統。奈拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。伊萊沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The city described a natural catastrophe, but authenticated directives showed deliberate political removals. Nari issued a concise public summary that distinguished confirmed facts from unresolved deaths. A courtyard in the lower district held a memorial with names missing from every official register. The ministry hoped a spectacular anniversary ceremony would dazzle citizens into overlooking the archive findings. The rain made the steep incline behind the tower slippery, prompting volunteers to close the footpath temporarily. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 城市把事件說成天災，認證命令卻顯示是刻意政治搬遷。 娜莉發表簡明公告，區分已確認事實與尚未釐清的死亡案例。 下層社區一座院子裡有紀念碑，刻著所有官方名冊都沒有的姓名。 部會希望華麗的週年慶能使市民目眩，忽略檔案發現。 雨水使塔後陡坡濕滑，志工因此暫時封閉步道。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Nara insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Eli drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。奈拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。伊萊畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Investigators found a transit locker containing duplicate tickets and letters from evacuees. An official film portrayed the outside settlements as medieval ruins, though their radio equipment was modern. The testimony described how forced relocation had separated families and reshaped experiences of motherhood. A pharmacy record from Harbor Nine matched medicines listed on an Everday evacuation train. Investigators agreed to publicize verified totals without exposing private medical histories. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 調查員找到一個運輸置物櫃，裡面有車票副本及撤離者書信。 官方影片把外部聚落拍成中世紀廢墟，但其無線電設備很現代。 證詞描述強制搬遷如何拆散家庭，改變許多人的母職經驗。 九號港的一份藥局紀錄，與永晝撤離列車名單上的藥物吻合。 調查員同意公布核實總數，不揭露私人病史。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The radiant artificial skyline could no longer conceal the living towns beyond the dome. The old administration's reliance on compulsory correction had concealed accountability for decades. Officials had labeled the lower district a slum to justify neglect, though families maintained schools and clinics there. The anniversary spectacle became meaningless once original relocation orders appeared in public. An old guard watched a child's dog wag its tail beside the sealed station and looked away in shame. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 明亮的人造天際線再也掩蓋不了穹頂外仍有人居住的城鎮。 舊政府依賴強制修正，讓責任追究被掩蓋數十年。 官員把下層社區稱作貧民窟，以合理化忽視，居民卻自己維持學校和診所。 原始搬遷命令公開後，週年慶的壯觀表演變得毫無意義。 一名老警衛看著孩子的狗在封閉車站旁搖尾巴，羞愧地轉過頭。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Nara passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Eli paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，奈拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。伊萊停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. Solven predicts chaos if the company loses exclusive control, and distributes a simulated blackout video.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 索爾文預言企業失去獨占後會混亂，散播模擬停電影片。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. Nara finds the simulation omitted functioning municipal backup systems.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 奈拉查出模擬刻意排除仍在運作的市政備援系統。


## CHAPTER 19｜The Invoice for a Childhood｜童年的帳單

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：857**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Nara refused to begin with the official conclusion. A parent receives a bill for years of sunlight consumed by a child before the subscription law existed. In the ring-shadow city, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。奈拉拒絕從官方結論開始。一位家長收到帳單，竟要追繳孩子在日照法出台前幾年使用的陽光。在環影城，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** Eli arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Nara could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 伊萊帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。奈拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The memorial boulevard displayed the names of survivors who had chosen public recognition. A shopkeeper served hot vegetable broth to crews working through the morning to clear storm debris from the harbor. The tired captain's pale complexion worried the nurse, who asked him to rest before returning to his vessel. A cosmetic repair to the painted railing could wait; structural cracks in the pier could not. A crater outside the old industrial plant appeared on aerial photographs omitted from founding lessons. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 紀念大道展示自願公開姓名的倖存者名單。 店主為整個上午清除港口風暴殘骸的工作隊送上熱蔬菜高湯。 疲憊船長蒼白的臉色讓護理師擔心，請他在回船前先休息。 欄杆油漆的表面修補可以等，碼頭的結構裂縫卻不能等。 舊工業廠外的一個坑洞出現在建城課程省略的空拍照片裡。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. The public prosecutor opens a separate investigation into unlawful retroactive billing. For a long moment no one spoke. Nara understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Eli broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。公訴人針對違法溯及收費另外立案調查。一時之間無人開口。奈拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。伊萊打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A surviving secretary recognized the dictation notes used to prepare the forced relocation orders. The old disease story had served as a diversion from the industrial land transfer. Nara could choose whether to enroll in the new family-contact program after reviewing its privacy rules. The evidence against the original council was formidable, but investigators still separated each person's actions. A gleam of clean water showed through the newly opened shallow pool window. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一名倖存祕書認出當年起草強制搬遷命令的口述筆記。 舊疾病故事曾用來轉移人們對工業土地轉讓的注意。 梅可以在閱讀隱私規範後，自行決定要不要加入新的家屬聯絡計畫。 針對舊議會的證據相當有力，調查員仍逐一區分個人行為。 新開的淺水池窗外透出乾淨水面的微光。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Solven demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Nara replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當索爾文總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。奈拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The new travel policy would remove a bureaucratic handicap that prevented unregistered residents from buying tickets. The conflict committee would intervene if a contract favored either lover unfairly. A lunar motif appeared in the institute's design brief. The children ate oatmeal before their morning reading session. From the outset, the reunion process required consent from both adults rather than a state order. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 新交通政策將移除阻止無戶籍居民購票的行政障礙。 若合約不公平地偏袒任一戀人，利益衝突委員會將介入。 學院設計簡報中出現月亮主題圖案。 孩子晨間閱讀前吃了燕麥粥。 家人重逢程序從一開始就要求兩名成年人同意，而非服從國家命令。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The exhausted diver was warned not to overdo the final inspection merely to meet an optimistic reopening schedule. The maintenance worker paused to sharpen a dull marking pencil before drawing the repair line. The usually sociable shopkeeper grew quiet while counting deliveries that could not reach families during the closure. Nara rejected the idea that engineers possessed moral superiority over the people whose lives they changed. No single victor could claim to have restored the city; thousands of people had preserved its evidence. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 團隊提醒疲憊潛水員不要為了樂觀的重開日程就過度工作。 維修工人先削尖鈍掉的標記鉛筆，再畫修繕線。 平時健談的店主在計算封航期間無法送到家庭的物資時安靜下來。 奈拉拒絕工程師在道德上優於其技術所影響之人的想法。 沒有單一勝利者可以自稱拯救城市；數千人共同保存了證據。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Nara resisted the urge to call it proof. Eli opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。奈拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。伊萊翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. A parent receives a bill for years of sunlight consumed by a child before the subscription law existed.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 一位家長收到帳單，竟要追繳孩子在日照法出台前幾年使用的陽光。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The public prosecutor opens a separate investigation into unlawful retroactive billing.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 公訴人針對違法溯及收費另外立案調查。


## CHAPTER 20｜The Hour the Array Stalled｜鏡陣停擺的一小時

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：858**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. A control fault halts the mirror array during a cold front, forcing public engineers and company staff to work together. Nara asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。寒流期間控制故障使鏡陣停止，公共工程師與企業員工被迫合作。奈拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Nara asked Eli to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。奈拉請伊萊說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The electricians installed an aluminum protective shield around an exposed backup cable before isolating a relay. The breadth of the medical review surprised officials who had expected only a narrow technical complaint. A coastal commonwealth offered neutral observers to verify that the city had stopped compulsory corrections. Residents of a crowded dormitory requested a supervised relay shutdown after reviewing their medical options. An independent editorial challenged the belief that public truth required compulsory exposure of private memories. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 電工隔離中繼器前，在裸露備援電纜周圍裝了鋁製防護罩。 醫療審查的廣泛程度讓官員驚訝，他們原以為只是狹窄技術申訴。 海岸共同體提供中立觀察員，核實城市已停止強制修正。 一棟擁擠宿舍的居民在了解醫療選項後，申請受監督的中繼停機。 一篇獨立社論質疑：公共真相是否需要強迫公開私人記憶。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. The emergency succeeds because they share technical information the contract had previously hidden. Nara felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。緊急修復成功，是因為他們公開分享過去合約不准透露的技術資訊。奈拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The coalition prohibited exclusion of residents who chose not to reveal their dream histories. The government's founding fable was losing power as residents read authenticated records. A fragrant bowl of soup reminded one survivor of home without requiring a forced memory session. A homosexual resident told the forum that personal identity and private memory both deserved protection. The original council had tried to industrialize the southern region by removing communities without consent. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 聯盟禁止排斥不願揭露夢境經歷的居民。 居民閱讀認證紀錄後，政府的建城寓言逐漸失去力量。 一碗香噴噴的湯讓一名倖存者想起家，不必接受強制恢復。 一名同性戀居民向論壇表示，個人身分與私人記憶都值得保護。 原議會曾試圖在未經同意下搬遷社區，以將南部地區工業化。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Nara had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Eli proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 奈拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。伊萊提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Ivy had grown over the sealed railway bridge, but its stone supports remained intact. A small bird found a perch above the public notice board where residents posted their own stories. An old petroleum pipeline beneath the relay station required inspection before electrical isolation began. Vale's talk of mental purity had disguised a policy of fear, surveillance, and compulsory editing. A minor quake damaged two supply cables, delaying an isolation that might otherwise endanger hospital patients. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 常春藤覆蓋了封閉鐵路橋，石製橋墩卻仍完好。 一隻小鳥棲在公告欄上方，居民正在下面張貼自己的故事。 中繼站下的一條舊石油管線，必須在電力隔離前接受檢查。 維爾口中的心智純淨，掩蓋了恐懼、監視與強制編輯政策。 一次小地震損壞兩條供電電纜，延後隔離程序，避免危及醫院病患。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Some residents threatened revolt against the new waiting rules until the reasons were explained. Rugged mechanical switches offered a safer emergency option than remotely controlled settings in damaged districts. Nara asked reporters not to skim the court ruling and turn its limited protection into an absolute claim. Children playing outdoors gave the lower neighborhood a vitality missing from official depictions of abandonment. Ivo continued to yearn for a reunion with his mother while honoring her request for time. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一些居民揚言反抗新等候規定，直到有人說明原因。 在設備受損地區，堅固的機械開關比遠端控制設定提供更安全的緊急選項。 奈拉請記者別草草瀏覽法院裁定，就把有限保障說成絕對結論。 在戶外玩耍的孩子，讓下層社區充滿活力，與官方描繪的荒廢景象不同。 伊沃仍渴望與母親重逢，同時尊重她需要時間的要求。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Nara returned to an unpaid sunlight meter. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Eli remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the ring-shadow city waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，奈拉再次看向一只欠費的陽光計量器。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。伊萊守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的環影城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。
