### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. A control fault halts the mirror array during a cold front, forcing public engineers and company staff to work together.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 寒流期間控制故障使鏡陣停止，公共工程師與企業員工被迫合作。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The emergency succeeds because they share technical information the contract had previously hidden.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 緊急修復成功，是因為他們公開分享過去合約不准透露的技術資訊。


## CHAPTER 21｜The Charter of an Open Sky｜開放天空憲章

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：860**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. The court voids unlawful monopoly provisions and requires public governance of essential light allocation. Nara checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。法院撤銷非法壟斷條款，要求基本日照分配受公共治理。奈拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Nara could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Eli suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，奈拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。伊萊提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The new clinic made every operator accountable to patients and an independent medical board. Nara used an analogy of a damaged library book to explain why restoration could not guarantee a perfect memory. The public auditorium hosted a debate over how many institutions should share custody of the archives. The clinic's cardinal rule was clear: consent could be withdrawn even after a session began. The nurse remained communicative throughout the procedure so the volunteer could understand each step. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 新診所讓每位操作人員都必須對病患與獨立醫療委員會負責。 奈拉以受損圖書作比喻，說明恢復不保證得到完美記憶。 公共禮堂舉辦辯論，討論幾個機構應共同保管檔案。 診所的首要規則很清楚：療程開始後仍可撤回同意。 護理師在整個療程中持續清楚溝通，讓志願者理解每個步驟。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. It retains safety regulation and a fair mechanism to fund maintenance rather than promising unlimited energy. Eli asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Nara agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。法院仍保留安全監管與公平維護費制度，沒有承諾無限能源。伊萊詢問是否可能只是出錯，奈拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Nari called the elderly teacher a comrade in reform without expecting her to reveal her private memories. A volunteer reported a hand cramp during testing, and the clinician immediately paused the session. The protocol required doctors to hospitalize any participant with serious adverse reactions rather than hide the event. The new uniform caused an itch for one cleaner, so the employer supplied an alternative. Long-term studies would be needed to understand the effects of correction pulses on health and longevity. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 娜莉稱年老教師是改革夥伴，卻不要求她揭露私人記憶。 一名志願者在測試時手部抽筋，醫師立刻暫停療程。 程序要求醫師遇到嚴重不良反應時安排住院，而非隱瞞事件。 新制服讓一名清潔員皮膚發癢，雇主因此提供替代款。 必須有長期研究才能理解修正脈衝對健康與壽命的影響。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Solven and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Nara rejected both. The issue was whether a private company may sell access to the natural sky, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住索爾文總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。奈拉兩者都拒絕。核心是私人企業是否有權出售人們接觸自然天空的機會，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The teacher's decision to stop helped propel the clinic toward a stronger culture of consent. A student wrote a piece of prose about learning to enter the water. The clinician rejected the claim that patients had gained psychic powers merely because they described similar dreams. An independent referee reviewed disputes between archive custodians and residents requesting access. Years of relentless overnight correction had left some patients unsure which memories were their own. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 教師決定停止療程，推動診所建立更強的同意文化。 一名學生寫了篇散文，描述學會入水的經驗。 臨床醫師拒絕說病患因描述相似夢境就獲得通靈能力。 一名獨立仲裁員審查檔案保管機構與申請查閱居民之間的爭議。 多年不停的夜間修正，使部分病患不確定哪些記憶屬於自己。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A relic of the founder's old office was displayed outside the guild meeting hall. Workers had to scrape loose paint away before applying the new protective coating. Visitors could scroll through public summaries without gaining access to anyone's sealed medical file. A child's red sneaker remained in a sealed evidence box because its owner had not consented to display. Officials had continued to underestimate residents' capacity to make informed decisions about their own memories. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 創辦人舊辦公室的遺物展示在公會會議廳外。 工人塗上新保護層前，必須刮除鬆動油漆。 訪客可以捲動閱讀公開摘要，卻不能看到任何人的密封病歷。 一隻兒童紅球鞋仍封存在證物箱裡，因為物主尚未同意展示。 官員一直低估居民對自身記憶做出知情選擇的能力。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Nara had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Eli wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Solven. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。奈拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。伊萊把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起索爾文總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The court voids unlawful monopoly provisions and requires public governance of essential light allocation.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 法院撤銷非法壟斷條款，要求基本日照分配受公共治理。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. It retains safety regulation and a fair mechanism to fund maintenance rather than promising unlimited energy.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 法院仍保留安全監管與公平維護費制度，沒有承諾無限能源。


## CHAPTER 22｜The Price That Finally Fell｜終於下降的價格

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：875**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Residents receive basic light without subscription while optional extra services are priced through public review. Nara knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。居民不訂閱也能取得基本日照，額外服務則經公開審查訂價。奈拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Nara asked Eli not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。奈拉請伊萊不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The archive contained an abundance of handwritten logs, though only a fraction had yet been carefully cataloged. The guardian asked the team to avoid causing unnecessary distress to survivors named in the recovered files. A resident's fury about hidden repair costs was understandable, but the meeting required precise questions rather than threats. A child's old drawing of a gorilla helped identify the school from which one evacuation group had departed. Poor access to the original logs would hamper future research, so the trustees funded a searchable public index. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 檔案室有大量手寫日誌，但目前只有一小部分完成仔細編目。 監護人要求小組避免讓檔案上出現的倖存者承受不必要痛苦。 居民對隱藏修繕費用的憤怒可以理解，會議仍要求精確提問而非威脅。 一張兒童大猩猩舊畫，協助辨認某批撤離者出發的學校。 無法取得原始日誌會妨礙未來研究，因此受託人資助可搜尋的公開索引。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Nara watches the first free dawn reach a school that once had to auction its garden. Nara watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Eli recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。奈拉看著第一道免費晨光照進曾不得不拍賣菜園的學校。奈拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。伊萊公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A rusted harmonica found beside the shipping letters was listed as property, not used to infer a life story. The archive's privacy policy protected heterosexual and LGBTQ families equally when recording relationships. The slow audit could irritate impatient activists, but it reduced the risk of accusing innocent workers. Some witnesses chose to remain mute in public and submit private written testimony instead. The forged epidemic order appeared to originate from a council office that had controlled the first relocations. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 運輸書信旁找到一把生鏽口琴，被列為物品，而沒有用來猜測一個人的人生。 檔案隱私政策在記錄親密關係時，同等保障異性戀與 LGBTQ 家庭。 緩慢稽核可能使焦急倡議者不耐煩，卻降低誤控無辜工人的風險。 部分證人選擇在公開場合保持沉默，改交私人書面證詞。 偽造的疫情命令似乎源自控制第一批搬遷的議會辦公室。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Nara insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Eli drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。奈拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。伊萊畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: An old transport photograph showed a pony cart beside the passenger train, helping date the station platform. The predecessor committee had failed to document how free hours were assigned. Residents could preview redacted archive summaries before deciding whether to request their own files. The original departure ledger was priceless to families seeking an accurate account of their relatives. Archivists worked to salvage water-damaged letters without replacing missing words with guesses. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 舊運輸照片裡，一輛小馬車停在客車旁，有助判定月台年代。 前任委員會未記錄免費時段如何分配。 居民可先預覽刪去敏感資訊的檔案摘要，再決定是否申請自己的紀錄。 對尋求親人真實去向的家庭而言，原始出發名冊無價。 檔案員努力搶救受潮書信，沒有用猜測補上缺失文字。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A sneaky change to the archive index had concealed the vault's location for decades. The archive allowed donors to choose whether their surname appeared beside a public description of family photographs. A sudden torrent of rain flooded the service stair, forcing the team to suspend the archive transfer. A retired veterinarian identified animal transport stamps on a set of railway cargo records. A violinist from the missing district offered a dated concert program as evidence of its public life. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一項偷偷修改檔案索引的行為，使地庫位置被藏了幾十年。 檔案室讓捐贈者選擇家族照片公開說明旁是否顯示姓氏。 一陣暴雨急流淹過維修樓梯，迫使小組暫停檔案移交。 退休獸醫認出一批鐵路貨運紀錄上的動物運輸印章。 一名來自失蹤行政區的小提琴家提供有日期的音樂會節目單，證明當地曾有公共生活。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Nara passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Eli paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，奈拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。伊萊停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Residents receive basic light without subscription while optional extra services are priced through public review.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 居民不訂閱也能取得基本日照，額外服務則經公開審查訂價。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Nara watches the first free dawn reach a school that once had to auction its garden.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 奈拉看著第一道免費晨光照進曾不得不拍賣菜園的學校。


## CHAPTER 23｜An Honest Maintenance Bill｜誠實的維修帳單

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：835**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. The new public board publishes actual orbital repair costs with independent engineering notes. Nara had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the ring-shadow city, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。新的公共理事會公布真實軌道維修成本與獨立工程師備註。奈拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在環影城，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Nara listened longer than the schedule allowed. Eli reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。奈拉聽得比議程允許的更久。伊萊提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The court separated the railway collision reports from later claims that every missing person had died. A child's comet drawing on a recovered school paper helped date the lesson using an astronomical calendar. Clear safety rules could deter risky shortcuts more effectively than scolding. An eccentric archivist had kept obsolete paper duplicates when everyone else trusted the central database. Court officers would escort endangered witnesses between the hearing room and protected accommodation. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 法院把鐵路碰撞事故報告，與後來宣稱所有失蹤者都死亡的說法分開。 一張復原學校作業上的彗星圖，協助用天文年曆判定課程年代。 明確安全規則可能比責罵更能遏止冒險捷徑。 一名古怪檔案員在大家只信任中央資料庫時，仍保存淘汰的紙本副本。 法院人員會護送受威脅證人往返聽證室與保護住所。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Families can challenge mistakes instead of losing daylight automatically over disputed fees. Nara read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Eli did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。家庭可以對錯帳提出異議，不再因費用爭議自動失去日照。奈拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。伊萊沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: An engineering examinee used the case to explain why safety reviews must include deployment conditions. A child's garment retained the stitched name of a school removed from official maps. The board separated navigation performance from ownership questions, although both would affect the tower's future. Nara's outlook changed from heroic rescue to the slower work of institutions that could be challenged. A surviving paperback novel contained a handwritten address from the vanished orchard district. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一名工程考生以本案說明，安全審查為何須包含實際部署條件。 一件童衣仍縫著已從官方地圖移除的學校名稱。 委員會把導航效能與所有權問題分開，儘管兩者都會影響燈塔未來。 奈拉的觀點從英雄拯救，轉向可接受質疑的慢速制度工作。 一本倖存的平裝小說裡，寫著消失果園行政區的手寫地址。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Director Solven demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Nara replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當索爾文總監要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。奈拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Fear of making another mistake could paralyze reform unless every decision remained open to review. The judge said a patriot was not someone who protected a government from evidence of wrongdoing. A curious child tried to peek behind the curtain during the vote count. A sorrowful account of a past shipwreck reminded the board why disputed safety records deserved serious attention. The old correction policy had created a spiral of fear, secrecy, and further technical intervention. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 如果每項決策不能接受審查，害怕再犯錯便可能使改革癱瘓。 法官說，愛國者不是替政府擋住不法證據的人。 一名好奇孩子在計票時試圖偷看布幕後方。 一段過去船難的悲傷敘述，提醒委員會為何必須認真面對有爭議的安全紀錄。 舊修正政策造成恐懼、保密與更多技術干預的惡性循環。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: No subordinate should be ordered to falsify a medical record, regardless of who holds authority. The new regulations forbade operators to transmit memory-altering pulses without individualized medical consent. The artificial stars continued to twinkle above the courthouse after the ruling was announced. An old velvet seat from the missing train was preserved as evidence, not turned into a propaganda display. Nara felt the train platform vibrate as engineers tested the repaired coastal track. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 不論上級權勢多大，都不能命令下屬偽造病歷。 新規禁止未取得個別醫療同意就傳送改變記憶的脈衝。 判決宣布後，人造星星仍在法院上方閃爍。 一張失蹤列車上的舊天鵝絨座椅被保留為證物，而非宣傳展示。 工程師測試修復的海岸鐵路時，奈拉感到月台震動。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Nara resisted the urge to call it proof. Eli opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。奈拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。伊萊翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. The new public board publishes actual orbital repair costs with independent engineering notes.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 新的公共理事會公布真實軌道維修成本與獨立工程師備註。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. Families can challenge mistakes instead of losing daylight automatically over disputed fees.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 家庭可以對錯帳提出異議，不再因費用爭議自動失去日照。


## CHAPTER 24｜The City Learns to Share｜城市學會分享

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：876**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. A festival redistributes mirrors and safe growing space to districts harmed by the old system. Nara saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。節慶把鏡子和安全種植空間重新分配給舊制度傷害的街區。奈拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** Eli arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Nara could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 伊萊帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。奈拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The real coastline extended beyond the walls farther than any public school map had shown. The new director would compile public reports from several village committees. The independent bridge inspection would complement the medical safeguards for family travelers. Nara did not try to console Nara with promises that the lost years could simply be recovered. The new travel charter barred officials from using forgotten family records to detain innocent passengers. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 真正的海岸線延伸到城牆外，比任何公立學校地圖畫得更遠。 新主任將彙整數個村委會的公開報告。 獨立橋梁檢查將補足探親旅客的醫療保障。 奈拉沒有試圖用「失去歲月能輕易找回」的承諾安慰梅。 新旅行章程禁止官員利用被遺忘的家屬紀錄拘留無辜乘客。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. Eli declines an offer to become the array’s sole commander, preferring a rotating team. For a long moment no one spoke. Nara understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Eli broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。伊萊拒絕當鏡陣唯一指揮官，選擇輪值團隊。一時之間無人開口。奈拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。伊萊打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Diplomacy between Everday and the local station made the first supervised passenger service possible. The audit traced a downward adjustment in the reported repair costs. the office council adopted drastic changes only after independent review. The authorities avoided a drastic reopening before the damaged bridges passed independent tests. The coastal museum displayed a glacier survey that proved the outside world still had active scientific institutions. Nara refused to treat the new coastal reports as gospel; they too required verification and public challenge. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 長壽特區與九號港之間的外交協商，使首班受監督客運成為可能。 辦公場所議會經獨立審查後才採取劇烈改革。 當局避免在受損橋梁通過獨立檢查前，進行激烈全面開放。 海岸博物館展示一份冰河測量，證明外部世界仍有運作中的科學機構。 奈拉拒絕把新的海岸報告當成絕對真理，它們也需要驗證與公開質疑。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Nara had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Eli proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 奈拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。伊萊提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Security staff had procedures to prevent anyone from attempting to hijack the first passenger train. Vale's supporters called Nara a hypocrite, but she had already accepted professional sanctions for her patch. A familiar station jingle played as the repaired train approached Harbor Nine. Ivo admitted he had felt lonesome for years, though he did not expect Nara to repair that loss for him. The new map printed accurate longitude markings for towns once erased from public navigation systems. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 保全人員有程序，防止有人企圖劫持首班客車。 維爾支持者稱奈拉虛偽，但她早已接受修補程式的專業處分。 修復列車接近九號港時，響起熟悉的車站短音樂。 伊沃承認多年來感到孤單，卻不期待奈拉替他修復所有失落。 新地圖為昔日從公共導航系統抹除的城鎮印上準確經度標記。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Above the rooftops, the mirror array rotated with silent precision. Nara could read its position from a shadow on a water tank. Sunlight was physical, not a promise on a billing screen. Eli wondered aloud how many children had learned to associate dawn with the sound of a payment warning.

**繁中** 屋頂上方，反射鏡陣無聲而精準地旋轉。奈拉可以從水塔陰影讀出它的位置。陽光是真實的物理現象，不是帳單螢幕上的承諾。伊萊低聲問，有多少孩子已把黎明和付款警告的聲音連在一起。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The monstrous scale of the old deception did not make its living victims into objects for public display. The reopening ceremony acknowledged the oppression that had kept families separated for decades. The newly surveyed reef remained marked on the chart even after the navigation beacon passed its tests. The two cities promised solidarity through practical rights, not a forced declaration of shared identity. The new commission could not become another vice gripping citizens' private memories in the name of safety. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 舊騙局規模再駭人，也不能把在世受害者變成公眾展示物。 重開儀式承認過去壓迫如何使家庭分離數十年。 即使導航燈通過測試，新測礁石仍保留標示在海圖上。 兩座城市承諾以實際權利展現團結，而非強迫宣告共同身分。 新委員會不能再以安全之名，成為箝制居民私人記憶的惡習與束縛。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Nara returned to an unpaid sunlight meter. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Eli remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the ring-shadow city waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，奈拉再次看向一只欠費的陽光計量器。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。伊萊守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的環影城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

| L6-0611 | `oppression` | n. | `oppression` | 壓迫 | The reopening ceremony acknowledged the oppression that had kept families separated for decades. |
| L6-0735 | `reef` | n. | `reef` | 礁石 | The newly surveyed reef remained marked on the chart even after the navigation beacon passed its tests. |
| L6-0836 | `solidarity` | n. | `solidarity` | 團結 | The two cities promised solidarity through practical rights, not a forced declaration of shared identity. |
| L6-0966 | `vice` | n. | `vice` | 惡習／束縛 | The new commission could not become another vice gripping citizens' private memories in the name of safety. |

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. A festival redistributes mirrors and safe growing space to districts harmed by the old system.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 節慶把鏡子和安全種植空間重新分配給舊制度傷害的街區。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. Eli declines an offer to become the array’s sole commander, preferring a rotating team.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 伊萊拒絕當鏡陣唯一指揮官，選擇輪值團隊。


## CHAPTER 25｜A Morning Without a Button｜沒有按鈕的早晨

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：863**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Nara refused to begin with the official conclusion. Nara wakes to sunlight reaching her room before any billing screen appears. In the ring-shadow city, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。奈拉拒絕從官方結論開始。奈拉醒來時陽光已照入房間，沒有任何帳單螢幕跳出。在環影城，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Nara asked Eli to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。奈拉請伊萊說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A swimmer with asthma asked for a lesson plan compatible with her clinician's advice. A bilateral agreement between Everday and the local station protected voluntary family visits and data privacy. The new director met her counterpart from another valley to coordinate emergency support. The coastal train used a backup diesel engine during sections where the electric line remained under repair. The clinic stopped using disposable sensor bands when reusable alternatives passed medical safety tests. Nara asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名氣喘泳者要求課程能配合臨床專業人員的建議。 長壽特區與九號港的雙邊協議，保障自願探親與資料隱私。 新站長與另一座山谷的對口主管協調緊急支援。 海岸列車行經電線仍在維修的路段時，使用備援柴油引擎。 可重複使用的感測環通過醫療安全測試後，診所不再使用拋棄式環帶。 奈拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. She knows the mirrors still need care, but the sun itself is no longer sold by the minute. Nara felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。她知道鏡陣仍需維護，但太陽本身不再按分鐘販售。奈拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Officials could no longer divert requests for missing-person records into a general category of sleep complaints. Nara could doze peacefully on the afternoon train without expecting an official alarm to punish her dreams. A friend joked that Ivo was Nara's fiancé, but Nara replied that they had made no such announcement. The archive trust depended on enforceable rules as well as goodwill among former opponents. The new archive used a hybrid system of physical originals and verified digital copies. Eli marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 官員不能再把尋找失蹤者紀錄的申請，轉送到一般睡眠申訴類別。 奈拉能在午後列車上安心打盹，不再預期官方警報會懲罰她的夢。 朋友開玩笑稱伊沃是奈拉未婚夫，奈拉回答兩人從未宣布訂婚。 檔案信託除了仰賴昔日對手的善意，也需要可執行的規則。 新檔案館採用紙本正本與可驗證數位副本的混合制度。 伊萊標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Director Solven and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Nara rejected both. The issue was whether a private company may sell access to the natural sky, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住索爾文總監的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。奈拉兩者都拒絕。核心是私人企業是否有權出售人們接觸自然天空的機會，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: An old lad from Orchard East returned as an elderly man to see the street restored to the map. The repaired first-aid case had a fresh lining beneath its worn outer cover. A handwritten manuscript of the neighborhood's history was added to the public library with its author's consent. Public outrage helped start the inquiry, but patient verification made the reforms durable. Nara saw the paradox of her profession: a technology built to relieve pain had been used to deny reality. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一名東果園昔日的少年如今已是老人，回來看街道重新出現在地圖上。 修好的急救箱在磨損外殼下換上新內襯。 一份手寫社區歷史稿在作者同意下納入公共圖書館。 公眾憤怒促成調查，耐心驗證才讓改革得以長久。 奈拉看見自己專業的悖論：為減輕痛苦而建的技術，竟被用來否認現實。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The town helped rehabilitate the old weather classroom so future keepers could train without closing the public path. A renowned historian helped verify the original railway dates but did not control the public archive. Residents chose to revive the orchard festival with new traditions rather than recreate an imagined perfect past. Underneath the new public map lay layers of streets whose histories the city finally allowed people to study. The agreement rejected forced unification of the cities and protected each community's self-government. A coastal villa once used by senior officials was converted into a public center for reunion counseling. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 小鎮協助修復舊氣象教室，讓未來守護員不必封閉公共步道也能受訓。 一位著名歷史學家協助核實原始鐵路日期，卻不掌控公共檔案館。 居民選擇以新傳統恢復果園節，而不是重建想像中完美的過去。 新公共地圖底下有一層又一層街道歷史，如今城市終於允許人們研究。 協議拒絕強制統一兩座城市，保障各社區自治。 一棟曾供高階官員使用的海岸別墅，改成公共家屬重逢諮詢中心。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Nara had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Eli wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Director Solven. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。奈拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。伊萊把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起索爾文總監注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

**EN** Nara did not call the day a perfect victory. She knows the mirrors still need care, but the sun itself is no longer sold by the minute. What endured was a decision that the people of the ring-shadow city could challenge, revise, and make their own. Eli placed an unpaid sunlight meter beside the public record. They left the last page open for names not yet written.

**繁中** 奈拉不把這一天稱為完美勝利。她知道鏡陣仍需維護，但太陽本身不再按分鐘販售。真正留下的是環影城人民有權質疑、修改並共同擁有的決定。伊萊把一只欠費的陽光計量器放在公共紀錄旁。他們把最後一頁留白，等待未來尚未寫上的姓名。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Nara wakes to sunlight reaching her room before any billing screen appears.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 奈拉醒來時陽光已照入房間，沒有任何帳單螢幕跳出。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. She knows the mirrors still need care, but the sun itself is no longer sold by the minute.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 她知道鏡陣仍需維護，但太陽本身不再按分鐘販售。
